Description

Thick Ada binding to the OpenCV Imgproc module. Every operation works on OpenCV.Core.Mat values and the shared value types of the root OpenCV package (Point, Size, Rect, Scalar, Border_Kind and the element value types); this package defines only Imgproc-specific types.

Conventions shared by all operations:

  • Images are borrowed for the duration of a call and never retained. A Mat of mode in is read and not modified; operations that modify an image take it as in out and say so.

  • Element types are written as a Core depth and channel count, so "UInt8 C3" is depth UInt8 with three channels. Images are two-dimensional unless stated otherwise.

  • Coordinates are zero-based. Point X is the column and Y the row; Size Width counts columns and Height counts rows.

  • A Region is a Mat header viewing part of another Mat. Operations that snapshot or isolate their inputs say so and treat a Region as its own logical image. Otherwise a Region is passed to OpenCV as a submatrix, and OpenCV's border extrapolation may read pixels of the parent just outside it (OpenCV looks outside a submatrix unless asked not to). Pixels outside a Region are written only where an operation says so.

  • Each operation states whether it returns fresh owning storage, replaces a Destination, or writes into existing storage, and which input and output aliases are supported.

  • Contract violations and failures reported by OpenCV raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error. A value outside an Ada subtype range raises Constraint_Error before the operation runs.

  • Behavior notes that depend on the OpenCV release refer to the reviewed OpenCV 4.1, 4.10 and 5.0 sources. Floating-point results retain native rounding; bit identity across OpenCV releases, platforms and optional acceleration backends is not promised unless stated.

Drawing primitives mutate Image in place. A shallow Mat alias or a Region shares that storage, so a drawing made through either header is visible through the other. Region coordinates are relative to the Region. Pixels outside a Region are not modified. Image must be nonempty and two-dimensional, with depth UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32, or Float64 and 1 through 4 channels. Scalar components are written in channel order; unused components are ignored and there is no alpha blending. The components used by Image.Channels must be finite. Coordinates may lie outside Image; OpenCV clips them. Outline operations use a positive Drawing_Thickness. Anti_Aliased_Line is accepted only for UInt8 images. Contract violations and OpenCV failures raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error.

Hough detection. Every operation takes a private continuous snapshot of Source before calling OpenCV, so Source pixels are never modified and a Region is analysed as its own logical image with its own borders. Results are Ada-owned copies. Result order is not part of the contract. Contract violations, native arithmetic limits, and OpenCV failures raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error.

Hough evidence. Votes are accumulator support counts. OpenCV keeps only strict local maxima whose count exceeds the requested threshold, so an accepted line or circle normally reports Votes > threshold. Magnitudes and ordering may differ between OpenCV generations and optional backends; use Votes for ranking and evidence, not as a portable constant.

Segmentation. Contract violations, native arithmetic limits, and OpenCV failures raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error.

Accumulate_Histogram

function Accumulate_Histogram
  (Base : Histogram; Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return Histogram

Returns a new histogram with Base's binning mode, source positions, channels and exact stored edges, and the Float32 sum of Base's bins and a fresh histogram of Source using those dimensions. Base must already be calculated, and every stored source position must be 0. Base, Source and (when present) Mask are not modified. The sum is checked in a wider type; a nonfinite, negative or non-Float32 sum rejects the whole update. Counts above 2 ** 24 may lose integer-unit precision because storage is Float32. This does not call native calcHist with accumulate=true.

Source follows the calculation contract of Base's binning mode (Calculate_Histogram or Calculate_Nonuniform_Histogram); its geometry need not match the images that produced Base.

Parameters
Base

Calculated histogram whose stored source positions are all 0; it supplies the axes and the counts to add to.

Source

Image sampled with Base's channels and bins.

Return Value

New Histogram holding the summed counts and Base's metadata.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for a default-initialized Base, a nonzero stored source position, any calculation error for Source, or a bin sum that is not a finite Float32.

Accumulate_Histogram

function Accumulate_Histogram
  (Base : Histogram; Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat; Mask : OpenCV.Core.Mat)
   return Histogram

Masked single-source Accumulate_Histogram: as the unmasked overload, but only pixels whose Mask value is nonzero are added. Mask follows the masked calculation contract: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 with Source's rows and columns. Base, Source and Mask are not modified.

Parameters
Base

Calculated histogram whose stored source positions are all 0; it supplies the axes and the counts to add to.

Source

Image sampled with Base's channels and bins.

Mask

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with Source's rows and columns; nonzero samples select pixels.

Return Value

New Histogram holding the summed counts and Base's metadata.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for any condition of the unmasked overload or an invalid Mask.

Accumulate_Histogram

function Accumulate_Histogram
  (Base : Histogram; Sources : OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array) return Histogram

Multi-source Accumulate_Histogram: returns a new histogram with Base's binning mode, source positions, channels and exact edges, and the Float32 sum of Base's bins and a fresh histogram of Sources, using Base's stored source positions, channels and complete boundary sequences. Every stored position must be below Sources'Length; position 0 is Sources'First. Sources within this call must share geometry and depth and follow the multi-source calculation contract of Base's binning mode. Separate accumulation calls need not share geometry with each other or with the images that produced Base. Base must be calculated; Base and Sources are not modified. A nonfinite, negative or non-Float32 sum rejects the whole update, and counts above 2 ** 24 may lose integer-unit precision. Native calcHist accumulation is not used.

Parameters
Base

Calculated histogram supplying the axes and the counts to add to.

Sources

Images sampled with Base's source positions, channels and bins.

Return Value

New Histogram holding the summed counts and Base's metadata.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for a default-initialized Base, any calculation error for Sources, or a bin sum that is not a finite Float32.

Accumulate_Histogram

function Accumulate_Histogram
  (Base    : Histogram;
   Sources : OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array;
   Mask    : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return Histogram

Masked multi-source Accumulate_Histogram: as the unmasked multi-source overload, but only locations whose Mask value is nonzero are added. Mask is a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 Mat with the common source rows and columns, applied at the same location of every source. Base, Sources and Mask are not modified.

Parameters
Base

Calculated histogram supplying the axes and the counts to add to.

Sources

Images sampled with Base's source positions, channels and bins.

Mask

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with the common source rows and columns; nonzero samples select locations.

Return Value

New Histogram holding the summed counts and Base's metadata.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for any condition of the unmasked multi-source overload or an invalid Mask.

Accumulate_Image

function Accumulate_Image
  (Source, Base : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Image/pixel accumulation, not histogram accumulation: returns Base + Source at every pixel and channel. Accumulate_Image, Accumulate_Image_Square, Accumulate_Image_Product and Update_Running_Average share this contract. All inputs are nonempty 2-D Mats of identical geometry. Base is Float32 or Float64 with Source's channel count. Plain accumulation accepts UInt8, UInt16, Float32 and Float64 (Float64 requires a Float64 Base), any channels. Square, product and running average accept UInt8/Float32 C1 or C3.

Each function updates a private Base clone and returns fresh independent storage, with Base's geometry, depth and channel count, only after success; nothing is modified on failure. Inputs and Region parents are never modified; Regions contribute only logical pixels, and a Base Region yields a packed independent result. All input storage aliases are supported, including identical and overlapping inputs. Native floating overflow is not saturated or prohibited: arithmetic follows IEEE, including overflow to infinity. Each input's pixel count, channel-sample count, byte count and row stride must not exceed 2_147_483_647 (native int layout limits).

Parameters
Source

UInt8, UInt16, Float32 or Float64 image (Float64 requires a Float64 Base) added to Base; any channel count.

Base

Float32 or Float64 accumulator of Source's geometry and channel count. It is read, not updated.

Return Value

Fresh Mat typed like Base holding Base + Source.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty or non-2-D input, mismatched geometry or channel count, an unsupported depth pair, an input beyond the native layout limits, or a native failure.

Accumulate_Image

function Accumulate_Image
  (Source, Base, Mask : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Masked Accumulate_Image: the result holds Base + Source only at pixels whose Mask sample is nonzero and Base's values elsewhere. Any nonzero Mask value enables every channel at that pixel. Depths, geometry, fresh-result publication, Region, aliasing and layout rules are those of the unmasked overload.

Parameters
Source

Image added at enabled pixels: UInt8, UInt16, Float32 or Float64 (Float64 requires a Float64 Base), any channel count.

Base

Float32 or Float64 accumulator of Source's geometry and channel count. It is read, not updated.

Mask

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with Source's rows and columns; zero preserves Base. It may share storage with Source.

Return Value

Fresh Mat typed like Base holding the masked sum.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source or Base as for the unmasked overload, or for a Mask that is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1 or of different geometry.

Accumulate_Image_Product

function Accumulate_Image_Product
  (Source_1, Source_2, Base : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Product accumulation: returns Base + Source_1 * Source_2 at every pixel and channel, in fresh storage typed like Base. The sources are UInt8 or Float32 C1 or C3 with identical depth, channel count and geometry; they may be the same Mat. Base is Float32 or Float64 with their geometry and channel count. Otherwise as Accumulate_Image: nonempty 2-D inputs, private Base clone published only after success, logical Regions, all aliases supported, native layout limits, and unsaturated IEEE floating arithmetic.

Parameters
Source_1

First UInt8 or Float32 C1 or C3 factor image.

Source_2

Second factor image, typed and sized like Source_1.

Base

Float32 or Float64 accumulator of the sources' geometry and channel count. It is read, not updated.

Return Value

Fresh Mat typed like Base holding the product sum.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty or non-2-D input, sources of different type or geometry, an unsupported depth or channel count, a mismatched Base, an input beyond the native layout limits, or a native failure.

Accumulate_Image_Product

function Accumulate_Image_Product
  (Source_1, Source_2, Base, Mask : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Masked Accumulate_Image_Product: adds Source_1 * Source_2 only at pixels whose Mask sample is nonzero; elsewhere the result keeps Base. Any nonzero Mask value enables every channel at that pixel. Otherwise as the unmasked overload.

Parameters
Source_1

First UInt8 or Float32 C1 or C3 factor image.

Source_2

Second factor image, typed and sized like Source_1.

Base

Float32 or Float64 accumulator of the sources' geometry and channel count. It is read, not updated.

Mask

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with the sources' rows and columns; zero preserves Base.

Return Value

Fresh Mat typed like Base holding the masked product sum.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for invalid sources or Base as for the unmasked overload, or for a Mask that is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1 or of different geometry.

Accumulate_Image_Square

function Accumulate_Image_Square
  (Source, Base : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Squared accumulation: returns Base + Source * Source at every pixel and channel, in fresh storage typed like Base. Source is UInt8 or Float32 with one or three channels; Base is Float32 or Float64 with Source's geometry and channel count. Otherwise as Accumulate_Image: nonempty 2-D inputs, private Base clone published only after success, logical Regions, all aliases supported, native layout limits, and unsaturated IEEE floating arithmetic.

Parameters
Source

UInt8 or Float32 C1 or C3 image whose squares are added.

Base

Float32 or Float64 accumulator of Source's geometry and channel count. It is read, not updated.

Return Value

Fresh Mat typed like Base holding the squared sum.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty or non-2-D input, mismatched geometry or channel count, an unsupported depth or channel count, an input beyond the native layout limits, or a native failure.

Accumulate_Image_Square

function Accumulate_Image_Square
  (Source, Base, Mask : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Masked Accumulate_Image_Square: adds Source * Source only at pixels whose Mask sample is nonzero; elsewhere the result keeps Base. Any nonzero Mask value enables every channel at that pixel. Otherwise as the unmasked overload.

Parameters
Source

UInt8 or Float32 C1 or C3 image whose squares are added at enabled pixels.

Base

Float32 or Float64 accumulator of Source's geometry and channel count. It is read, not updated.

Mask

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with Source's rows and columns; zero preserves Base. It may share storage with Source.

Return Value

Fresh Mat typed like Base holding the masked squared sum.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source or Base as for the unmasked overload, or for a Mask that is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1 or of different geometry.

Adaptive_Block_Size

subtype Adaptive_Block_Size is Positive range 3 .. 2_147_483_647;

Side length, in pixels, of the square neighborhood used by Apply_Adaptive_Threshold. The subtype admits values from 3; the value must also be odd, and an even value raises OpenCV.OpenCV_Error.

Adaptive_Threshold_Method

type Adaptive_Threshold_Method is (Mean, Gaussian);

Local mean that Apply_Adaptive_Threshold computes over the Block_Size by Block_Size neighborhood of each pixel.

Enumeration Literal
Mean

Arithmetic mean of the neighborhood.

Gaussian

Gaussian-weighted mean of the neighborhood.

Adaptive_Threshold_Mode

type Adaptive_Threshold_Mode is (Binary, Binary_Inverse);

Output rule of Apply_Adaptive_Threshold for a pixel compared with its local threshold (local mean minus Bias), with maximum value M.

Enumeration Literal
Binary

M for pixels above the local threshold, otherwise 0.

Binary_Inverse

0 for pixels above the local threshold, otherwise M.

Affine_Mapping_Direction

subtype Affine_Mapping_Direction is Warp_Mapping_Direction;

Warp_Mapping_Direction under the name used by Warp_Affine; the values and their meaning are identical.

Apply_Adaptive_Threshold

procedure Apply_Adaptive_Threshold
  (Source        : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination   : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Block_Size    : Adaptive_Block_Size;
   Method        : Adaptive_Threshold_Method := Mean;
   Mode          : Adaptive_Threshold_Mode := Binary;
   Bias          : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 0.0;
   Maximum_Value : OpenCV.UInt8_Value := 255)

Applies an adaptive binary threshold to a non-empty, two-dimensional, UInt8, single-channel Source. Block_Size must be odd. OpenCV calculates the local threshold as the arithmetic neighborhood mean or a Gaussian-weighted neighborhood mean, according to Method, minus Bias. Bias must be finite; it may be positive, zero, or negative and is passed directly to OpenCV. Maximum_Value is a UInt8 value, including zero. Destination is replaced with a UInt8 single-channel Mat having Source's rows and columns, as a direct native output: OpenCV writes into its storage when it already has that size and type, so shallow copies and a Region's parent see the result, and otherwise rebinds it to new storage. Source remains unchanged when distinct from Destination; direct in-place operation is supported. OpenCV controls neighborhood border handling internally.

Parameters
Source

UInt8 C1 image to binarize.

Destination

Receives the UInt8 C1 binary image; may be the same Mat as Source.

Block_Size

Odd side length, in pixels, of the neighborhood.

Method

Local mean weighting; defaults to Mean.

Mode

Output rule; defaults to Binary.

Bias

Finite value subtracted from the local mean to form the local threshold; defaults to 0.0.

Maximum_Value

Value written for selected pixels; defaults to 255.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 or not single-channel, when Block_Size is even, when Bias is not finite, or when OpenCV fails.

Apply_Automatic_Threshold

procedure Apply_Automatic_Threshold
  (Source             : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination        : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Computed_Threshold : out OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Method             : Automatic_Threshold_Method := Otsu;
   Mode               : Threshold_Mode := Binary;
   Maximum_Value      : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 255.0)

Applies Otsu or Triangle automatic thresholding to a non-empty, two-dimensional, single-channel Source. Maximum_Value must be finite. Otsu accepts UInt8 and UInt16 Sources; Triangle accepts UInt8 only. OpenCV before 4.4 rejects UInt16 Otsu with OpenCV_Error. The result equals Apply_Threshold with Computed_Threshold, Mode and Maximum_Value. Destination is replaced with a single-channel Mat having Source's rows, columns, and depth, as a direct native output: OpenCV writes into its storage when it already has that size and type, so shallow copies and a Region's parent see the result, and otherwise rebinds it to new storage. Computed_Threshold receives OpenCV's selected value on success. Source remains unchanged. Maximum_Value affects only Binary and Binary_Inverse; it is ignored by the other threshold modes.

Parameters
Source

Single-channel image from which the threshold is selected and to which it is applied.

Destination

Receives the thresholded image.

Computed_Threshold

Receives the threshold OpenCV selected, in Source's sample units; assigned only on success.

Method

Threshold selection algorithm; defaults to Otsu.

Mode

Per-sample rule applied with the selected threshold; defaults to Binary.

Maximum_Value

Value written by Binary and Binary_Inverse; defaults to 255.0.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not single-channel or of a depth Method does not accept, when Maximum_Value is not finite, or when OpenCV fails.

Apply_Color_Map

procedure Apply_Color_Map
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Lookup_Table : OpenCV.Core.Mat)

Maps Source through a caller-supplied BGR table with native cv::applyColorMap. Source, its grayscale conversion, the fresh UInt8 C3 BGR result and the publication of Destination only after success are as for the Built_In_Color_Map overload. Lookup_Table must be 2-D UInt8 C3, 256 rows x 1 column (not 1x256). Row I contains the BGR triple for grayscale byte I, I = 0 .. 255. Custom C1 tables are intentionally excluded: OpenCV 4.1 returns C3 with them, whereas 4.10/5 return C1. The table is also snapshotted; noncontiguous Regions and Destination = Lookup_Table are supported, as are Destination = Source and Source/table shared storage. No scaling or interpolation is added.

Parameters
Source

Image to map: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 (table indices) or C3 (BGR, converted to grayscale first).

Destination

Receives the fresh UInt8 C3 BGR result with Source's rows and columns; unchanged on failure. It may be Source or Lookup_Table.

Lookup_Table

Color table: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C3 of 256 rows and 1 column, row I holding the B, G, R output for grayscale byte I.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8, or not C1 or C3; when Lookup_Table is not a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C3 Mat of 256 rows and 1 column; when Rows * Columns * 3 exceeds 2_147_483_647 or a snapshot span is not addressable; or when OpenCV fails.

Apply_Color_Map

procedure Apply_Color_Map
  (Source      : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Map         : Built_In_Color_Map)

Maps Source through a built-in colormap with native cv::applyColorMap. Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 or C3 Source. C1 bytes index the table; C3 is BGR converted to grayscale first, NOT independent B/G/R maps. Fresh owning UInt8 C3 BGR result with Source geometry. Logical-row snapshots isolate Regions and permit Destination = Source. Publication occurs only after success; failures raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error and leave Destination unchanged. Separate input storage is never modified. Destination is rebound to the result; its previous storage, shallow copies and a Region's parent are not written.

Parameters
Source

Image to map: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 (table indices) or C3 (BGR, converted to grayscale first).

Destination

Receives the fresh UInt8 C3 BGR result with Source's rows and columns; unchanged on failure. It may be Source.

Map

Built-in table to apply.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8, or not C1 or C3; when Rows * Columns * 3 exceeds 2_147_483_647 or a snapshot span is not addressable; or when OpenCV fails.

Apply_Morphology

procedure Apply_Morphology
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Operation    : Morphology_Operation;
   Kernel       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the centered Kernel_Size Apply_Morphology, but with a caller-supplied structuring element anchored at (Kernel.Columns / 2, Kernel.Rows / 2). Kernel is a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask: zero excludes, every nonzero value includes, without weighting; all-zero masks are rejected. Even, non-square and non-contiguous Region kernels are accepted; only the kernel's logical view participates and Kernel is not modified. Kernel and Destination must not overlap; Source/Destination in-place is supported, including a parent-backed Region. Border, Destination and native-limit behavior are those of the Kernel_Size overload.

Parameters
Source

Image to transform.

Destination

Receives the result of Operation (direct native output with Source's geometry and element type).

Operation

Compound operation to perform.

Kernel

Structuring element: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask whose nonzero elements are included.

Iterations

Repetitions of each constituent erosion and dilation; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source or Kernel, an all-zero Kernel, Kernel storage overlapping Destination, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;

Apply_Morphology

procedure Apply_Morphology
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Operation    : Morphology_Operation;
   Kernel_Size  : OpenCV.Size;
   Shape        : Morphology_Shape := Rectangle;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

Apply_Morphology performs Opening, Closing, Gradient, Top_Hat, or Black_Hat with a temporary structuring element of Kernel_Size and Shape, anchored at (Width / 2, Height / 2). Each constituent erosion and dilation is repeated Iterations times. Source must be a non-empty two-dimensional Mat of depth UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32, or Float64; channels are processed independently. Kernel dimensions must be positive, but need not be odd, and Iterations is at least one. Constant_Border, Replicate, Reflect, and Reflect_101 are supported; Wrap is rejected. Constant_Border uses Border_Value, by default OpenCV's neutral morphology border value. Destination receives Source's geometry and element type as a direct native output, as for Erode. Source remains unchanged unless it is also Destination, which is supported in-place. A Region is processed as its own logical image; pixels in its parent outside the view do not participate. The native signed-int limits of Erode apply.

Parameters
Source

Image to transform.

Destination

Receives the result of Operation.

Operation

Compound operation to perform.

Kernel_Size

Width (columns) and height (rows) of the generated structuring element; both must be positive.

Shape

Generated element shape; defaults to Rectangle.

Iterations

Repetitions of each constituent erosion and dilation; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, a zero kernel dimension, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Apply_Morphology

procedure Apply_Morphology
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Operation    : Morphology_Operation;
   Kernel_Size  : OpenCV.Size;

   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;

   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the centered Kernel_Size Apply_Morphology, but with an explicit Anchor inside the Kernel_Size box, where (0, 0) is the top-left element pixel; no negative center sentinel exists. A generated Cross passes through Anchor; Rectangle and Ellipse masks do not depend on Anchor, but their footprint shifts with it. The same Anchor is used by every constituent erosion and dilation. Source, Destination, border, in-place, Region and native-limit behavior are those of the centered overload.

Generated element shape; no default in this overload.

Element position aligned with each output pixel; it must lie inside the element.

Iterations

Repetitions of each constituent erosion and dilation; defaults to 1.

Raised exceptions

Raised for an invalid Source, a zero kernel dimension, an Anchor outside the element, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Apply_Threshold

procedure Apply_Threshold
  (Source          : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination     : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Threshold_Value : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Mode            : Threshold_Mode := Binary;
   Maximum_Value   : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 255.0)

Applies a fixed threshold independently to every channel of Source, using the per-sample rule selected by Mode (see Threshold_Mode). Source must be non-empty, two-dimensional, and UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32, or Float64; any channel count is accepted. Threshold_Value and Maximum_Value must be finite. Destination receives Source's rows, columns, depth, and channel count as a direct native output: OpenCV writes into its storage when it already has that size and type, so shallow copies and a Region's parent see the result, and otherwise rebinds it to new storage. Source remains unchanged. Maximum_Value affects only Binary and Binary_Inverse; it is ignored by Truncate, To_Zero, and To_Zero_Inverse.

Parameters
Source

Image whose samples are compared with Threshold_Value.

Destination

Receives the thresholded image.

Threshold_Value

Threshold compared with every sample, in Source's sample units.

Mode

Per-sample rule; defaults to Binary.

Maximum_Value

Value written by Binary and Binary_Inverse; defaults to 255.0.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D or of another depth, when Threshold_Value or Maximum_Value is not finite, or when OpenCV fails.

Algorithm Apply_Automatic_Threshold uses to select the threshold from a single-channel Source.

Back_Project

function Back_Project
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Distribution : Histogram;
   Scale        : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 1.0) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Returns a new Mat with Source's rows and columns and depth and one channel, holding for each pixel the Distribution bin value of its selected channels multiplied by Scale (saturated for UInt8 and UInt16), or 0 when a sample lies outside a bin. Uniform and nonuniform Distributions both use the edges stored in Distribution; callers do not resupply them. Every stored source position must be 0; a histogram that selects another source raises OpenCV_Error. Source must be a nonempty two-dimensional UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 Mat containing every stored channel; Scale must be finite. A Region is back-projected in view coordinates. Source and Distribution are not modified.

The result is fresh owning storage published only after success. Scale must also have a magnitude of at most OpenCV.Float32_Value'Last. For UInt8 and UInt16 results the product is formed in Float32 and rounded; Float32 results are not saturated. The Source size, stride and Float32 sample rules of calculation apply, as does the UInt8 nonuniform edge limit.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 image whose pixels are looked up.

Distribution

Calculated histogram whose stored channels and edges select the bins.

Scale

Finite factor applied to each bin value; defaults to 1.0.

Return Value

Fresh one-channel Mat of Source's size and depth.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for a default-initialized Distribution, a nonzero stored source position, an invalid Scale, an invalid Source or missing channel, a native int or edge limit, a nonfinite Float32 sample, or a native failure.

Back_Project

function Back_Project
  (Sources      : OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array;
   Distribution : Histogram;
   Scale        : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 1.0) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Back-projects Sources through the source positions and channels stored in Distribution. Sources must be nonempty, share rows, columns and a supported depth, and contain every stored Source_Position. The result has the first source's rows, columns and depth, and one channel. Sources and Distribution are not modified. Array bounds do not affect stored positions: position 0 is Sources'First.

Each location receives the bin value addressed by its selected samples multiplied by Scale, or 0 when a sample lies outside a bin. Uniform and nonuniform Distributions use their stored edges. Scale must be finite with a magnitude of at most OpenCV.Float32_Value'Last; UInt8 and UInt16 results are rounded and saturated. Sources are nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 Mats whose selected channels exist; Regions are their logical views. The result is fresh owning storage published only after success; the size, stride and Float32 sample rules of calculation apply, as does the UInt8 nonuniform edge limit.

Parameters
Sources

Images looked up at the same row and column.

Distribution

Calculated histogram whose stored source positions, channels and edges select the bins.

Scale

Finite factor applied to each bin value; defaults to 1.0.

Return Value

Fresh one-channel Mat with the first source's size and depth.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for a default-initialized Distribution, an invalid Scale, an empty or invalid Sources array, a missing source position or channel, a native int or edge limit, a nonfinite Float32 sample, or a native failure.

Background_Label

Background_Label : constant Component_Label := 0;

Label of every background (zero) Source pixel. The background has no entry in a Connected_Component_Set, so Get_Component rejects it.

Ballard_Template_Match

type Ballard_Template_Match is record
   Center : OpenCV.Float32_Point;
   Votes  : Natural;
end record;

One Find_Ballard_Template_Matches detection: where the Template center lies in the Scene and how much accumulator support that translation has.

Record fields
Center

Scene-local position, in pixels, of the integer Template center (Template.Columns / 2, Template.Rows / 2). Native values lie on the integer pixel lattice and are finite and nonnegative; binary32 cannot represent every integer above 2**24.

Votes

Native Int32 accumulator votes, decoded exactly and never through Float32; strictly greater than the Vote_Threshold used.

Ballard_Template_Match_Array

  array (Natural range <>) of Ballard_Template_Match;

Result of Find_Ballard_Template_Matches. Nonempty results are zero-based; an empty result has range 1 .. 0. Element order is not a ranking.

Bayer_Pattern

type Bayer_Pattern is (RGGB, GRBG, BGGR, GBRG);

Physical 2x2 color filter array tile at logical Source (0,0), named row by row: the first two letters are the colors at columns 0 and 1 of row 0, the last two those of row 1. These are physical names, not OpenCV's short code names: RGGB, GRBG, BGGR and GBRG select OpenCV's BayerBG, BayerGB, BayerRG and BayerGR codes respectively. Cropping at an odd row or column offset changes the phase; the caller supplies the pattern at the Region's own (0,0).

Enumeration Literal
RGGB

Row 0 is R, G; row 1 is G, B.

GRBG

Row 0 is G, R; row 1 is B, G.

BGGR

Row 0 is B, G; row 1 is G, R.

GBRG

Row 0 is G, B; row 1 is R, G.

Bilateral_Diameter

subtype Bilateral_Diameter is Positive range 1 .. 2_147_483_647;

Explicit neighborhood diameter, in pixels, for Bilateral_Filter. The subtype excludes OpenCV's zero or negative automatic-diameter sentinel; any positive value, odd or even, is accepted.

Bilateral_Filter

procedure Bilateral_Filter
  (Source      : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Sigma_Color : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Sigma_Space : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Border      : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Reflect_101)

Bilateral_Filter replaces Destination with an edge-preserving smoothing of Source (native cv::bilateralFilter). Spatial distance and pixel/color distance both weight the neighborhood average, so strong intensity or color discontinuities are preserved more than with a linear blur. Source must be a non-empty two-dimensional Mat of depth UInt8 or Float32 with exactly 1 or 3 channels. For 3-channel Sources, color-distance weighting uses the combined color difference rather than filtering channels independently. This overload asks OpenCV to derive the neighborhood diameter from Sigma_Space; there is no public zero or negative diameter sentinel.

Destination receives Source's rows, columns, depth, and channel count as a direct native output: storage that already has that size and type is reused (shallow copies and a Region's parent see the result); otherwise Destination is rebound to new storage. Direct in-place operation is not supported: Destination being Source, or a header whose data starts at Source's first element, is rejected. Source remains unchanged when Destination is distinct.

Parameters
Source

Image to smooth.

Destination

Receives the filtered image; must not alias Source.

Sigma_Color

Standard deviation of the intensity/color-distance weight; a small value preserves discontinuities more. Positive and finite.

Sigma_Space

Standard deviation of the spatial-distance weight, in pixels; it also determines the neighborhood diameter. Positive and finite.

Border

Extrapolation outside Source: Constant_Border, Replicate, Reflect, or Reflect_101 (default). Wrap is rejected.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty, non-2-D, or unsupported Source, a nonpositive or nonfinite sigma, Wrap, a Destination aliasing Source, or a failure reported by OpenCV.

Bilateral_Filter

procedure Bilateral_Filter
  (Source      : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Diameter    : Bilateral_Diameter;
   Sigma_Color : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Sigma_Space : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Border      : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Reflect_101)

This overload is the same Bilateral_Filter operation with an explicit neighborhood Diameter. Diameter is a positive integer and need not be odd. OpenCV uses Diameter as the neighborhood size independently of Sigma_Space; Sigma_Space still weights samples inside that neighborhood. Source requirements, borders, and the direct native Destination output match the automatic overload. Direct in-place operation is not supported.

Parameters
Source

UInt8 or Float32 image with 1 or 3 channels to smooth.

Destination

Receives the filtered image with Source's geometry and type; must not alias Source.

Diameter

Neighborhood diameter in pixels; any positive value.

Sigma_Color

Standard deviation of the intensity/color-distance weight; positive and finite.

Sigma_Space

Standard deviation of the spatial-distance weight in pixels, applied inside the Diameter neighborhood; positive and finite.

Border

Extrapolation outside Source: Constant_Border, Replicate, Reflect, or Reflect_101 (default). Wrap is rejected.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, a nonpositive or nonfinite sigma, Wrap, a Destination aliasing Source, or a failure reported by OpenCV.

Blend_Linear

function Blend_Linear
  (Source_1, Source_2, Weight_1, Weight_2 : OpenCV.Core.Mat)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Native cv::blendLinear: a per-pixel weighted blend of two images, returned as fresh independent owning storage with the sources' rows, columns, depth and channel count. The native binary32 formula per channel is

(Source_1 * Weight_1 + Source_2 * Weight_2)
  / (Weight_1 + Weight_2 + 1.0e-5f)

One weight per pixel from each map applies to all channels. Weights need not sum to one: (1, 1) approximates the average and (1, 0) retains the epsilon bias. UInt8 then uses native rounding/saturation. Both-zero weights yield zero for finite sources; Float32 NaN, Inf and overflow retain native IEEE behavior (including Inf * 0). Float32 cross-platform bit identity is not promised.

Sources: nonempty 2-D UInt8 or Float32 with identical depth, channels and geometry. Any Core-valid channel count is accepted when Columns * Channels <= INT_MAX (native representation allows up to 512 channels in OpenCV 4.1/4.10 and 128 in 5.0). Weights: nonempty 2-D Float32 C1 with the sources' geometry, every sample finite and in the range 0.0 .. 1.0.

All inputs remain unchanged; read-sharing/overlapping aliases work. Regions contribute only logical pixels, with original row strides. Concurrent external mutation is unsupported. Publication is atomic.

Parameters
Source_1

First image, weighted by Weight_1.

Source_2

Second image, weighted by Weight_2; same depth, channel count and geometry as Source_1.

Weight_1

Float32 C1 per-pixel weights for Source_1.

Weight_2

Float32 C1 per-pixel weights for Source_2.

Return Value

Fresh owning Mat with the blended pixels, typed like the sources.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty, non-2-D, mismatched or unsupported input, a weight sample that is nonfinite or outside 0.0 .. 1.0, an oversized channel-expanded width, or a native failure.

Box_Blur

procedure Box_Blur
  (Source      : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel_Size : OpenCV.Size;
   Border      : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Reflect_101)

Box_Blur replaces each Source pixel with the normalized average of its Kernel_Size neighborhood (native cv::blur): every coefficient is 1 / (Width * Height). Source must be a non-empty two-dimensional Mat with depth UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32, or Float64. Channels are processed independently and are not restricted. The kernel uses OpenCV's centered default anchor; no Anchor parameter is exposed.

Destination receives Source's rows, columns, depth, and channel count as a direct native output: storage that already has that size and type is reused (shallow copies and a Region's parent see the result); otherwise Destination is rebound to new storage. Direct in-place operation (Destination is Source) is supported; Source remains unchanged when distinct from Destination.

Parameters
Source

Image to average.

Destination

Receives the box-filtered image.

Kernel_Size

Neighborhood width and height in pixels; both must be positive and need not be odd or equal.

Border

Extrapolation outside Source: Constant_Border (zero samples), Replicate, Reflect, or Reflect_101 (default). Wrap is rejected.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty, non-2-D, or unsupported-depth Source, a zero kernel dimension, Wrap, or a failure reported by OpenCV.

Build_Gaussian_Pyramid

function Build_Gaussian_Pyramid
  (Source      : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Level_Count : Positive;
   Border      : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Reflect_101)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array

Build_Gaussian_Pyramid returns Level_Count images indexed 0 .. Level_Count - 1 (native cv::buildPyramid). Level 0 is an independent copy of Source; each later level is Pyramid_Down of the previous level with Border. Every level owns independent storage shared with neither Source nor another level. Source follows the Pyramid_Down contract (UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32, or Float64; any channel count). A Region is processed as its own logical image (it is cloned first); parent pixels never participate. The whole chain is checked for native signed-int overflow before any level is built.

Parameters
Source

Image that forms level 0.

Level_Count

Total number of levels, including level 0; must not exceed Maximum_Pyramid_Level_Count (Source).

Border

Extrapolation for every downsampling step: Replicate, Reflect, Reflect_101 (default), or Wrap. Constant_Border is rejected.

Return Value

Fresh array of Level_Count independent Mats, finest first.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, Constant_Border, a Level_Count above the distinct level count, an unsafe geometry, or a failure reported by OpenCV.

Build_Laplacian_Pyramid

function Build_Laplacian_Pyramid
  (Source      : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Level_Count : Positive;
   Border      : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Reflect_101;
   Precision   : Laplacian_Precision := Automatic_Precision)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array

Build_Laplacian_Pyramid converts Source to the selected floating depth (a private copy of its logical pixels), builds a Gaussian pyramid G (0 .. N) with Border, where N = Level_Count - 1, and returns L (0 .. N) where, for I < N,

L (I) = G (I) - Pyramid_Up (G (I + 1),
                            Output_Size => size of G (I))

and the last level is the low-frequency base L (N) = G (N), not a residual. Pyramid_Up always uses OpenCV's default border. All levels are Float32 or Float64 with Source's channel count and independent storage, so callers may edit residuals before reconstruction. Source, Region, Border, and Level_Count contracts match Build_Gaussian_Pyramid.

Parameters
Source

Image to decompose.

Level_Count

Total number of levels including the base; must not exceed Maximum_Pyramid_Level_Count (Source).

Border

Extrapolation of the Gaussian downsampling steps: Replicate, Reflect, Reflect_101 (default), or Wrap. Constant_Border is rejected.

Precision

Working floating depth of every level; Automatic_Precision by default.

Return Value

Fresh array indexed 0 .. Level_Count - 1: residuals finest first, then the low-frequency base.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, Constant_Border, a Level_Count above the distinct level count, an unsafe geometry, or a failure reported by OpenCV.

Built_In_Color_Map

Magma_Map

Native COLORMAP_MAGMA table.

Inferno_Map

Native COLORMAP_INFERNO table.

Plasma_Map

Native COLORMAP_PLASMA table.

Calculate_Histogram

function Calculate_Histogram
  (Source     : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Mask       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Dimensions : Histogram_Dimension_Array) return Histogram

Masked single-source Calculate_Histogram: as the unmasked overload, counting only pixels whose Mask value is nonzero. Mask must be a nonempty two-dimensional UInt8 C1 Mat with Source's rows and columns; it may share storage with Source. Mask is not modified. Masked-out Float32 samples are not inspected. The Mask row stride must fit native int.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 image to sample.

Mask

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with Source's rows and columns; nonzero samples select pixels.

Dimensions

Axes in dimension order (any index bounds), each selecting a Source channel and its uniform bins.

Return Value

Fresh immutable Histogram of the selected pixels' counts.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for any condition of the unmasked overload or for a Mask that is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1, of different geometry, or with an excessive row stride.

Calculate_Histogram

function Calculate_Histogram
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat; Dimensions : Histogram_Dimension_Array)
   return Histogram

Counts Source samples into a new dense histogram (no accumulation, no normalization). Source must be a nonempty two-dimensional UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 Mat, every Channel must be below Source.Channels, and Dimensions'Length must be 1 .. Maximum_Histogram_Dimensions. A Region is treated as the whole image. Source is not modified. Every stored source position is 0.

Each pixel adds one to the bin addressed by its selected samples, only when every selected sample falls in a bin of its axis. The result has Uniform_Binning, the given channels and ranges, and fresh Float32 counts published only after success. Each dimension needs finite bounds with Lower_Bound < Upper_Bound, and the product of all bin counts must not exceed 2_147_483_647. Native int limits also apply: Rows times Columns and the row stride in elements must fit int; for UInt8 and UInt16 the bin coordinates of 0 and of the largest sample (255 or 65535) must lie inside int; every evaluated Float32 sample must be finite and its bin coordinate (Sample - Lower_Bound) * Bin_Count / (Upper_Bound - Lower_Bound) must lie inside int. Finite samples outside the bins remain legal.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 image to sample.

Dimensions

Axes in dimension order (any index bounds), each selecting a Source channel and its uniform bins.

Return Value

Fresh immutable Histogram of Float32 counts.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, a missing channel, a dimension count outside 1 .. 10, invalid bounds, an excessive bin product, a native int limit, a nonfinite or out-of-range Float32 sample, or a native failure.

Calculate_Histogram

function Calculate_Histogram
  (Sources    : OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array;
   Mask       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Dimensions : Histogram_Source_Dimension_Array) return Histogram

Masked multi-source Calculate_Histogram: as the unmasked overload, counting only locations whose Mask value is nonzero. Mask must be a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 Mat with the common source geometry. It is applied at the same (row, column) of every source and may share storage with a source. Mask is not modified. Masked-out Float32 samples are not inspected.

Parameters
Sources

Images sampled at the same row and column; position 0 is Sources'First.

Mask

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with the common source rows and columns; nonzero samples select locations.

Dimensions

Axes in dimension order (any index bounds), each selecting a source position, a channel and its uniform bins.

Return Value

Fresh immutable joint Histogram of the selected locations.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for any condition of the unmasked overload or for a Mask that is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1, of different geometry, or with an excessive row stride.

Calculate_Histogram

function Calculate_Histogram
  (Sources    : OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array;
   Dimensions : Histogram_Source_Dimension_Array) return Histogram

Joint histogram of every Sources element. Sources must be nonempty and may have any array bounds. Each element must be a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 Mat; every element must share the first element's rows, columns and depth. Channel counts may differ. Each dimension's Source_Position is a zero-based iteration position (not the Ada index) and must be below Sources'Length; its Channel must exist on that source. Dimensions'Length is 1 .. Maximum_Histogram_Dimensions. No source is modified. A Region is its logical view.

A location is counted only when every selected sample falls in a bin of its axis. The result has Uniform_Binning and stores each axis's source position, channel and range; its fresh Float32 counts are published only after success. Bounds, bin product, native int and Float32 sample rules are those of the single-source overload, applied to every selected sample.

Parameters
Sources

Images sampled at the same row and column; position 0 is Sources'First.

Dimensions

Axes in dimension order (any index bounds), each selecting a source position, a channel and its uniform bins.

Return Value

Fresh immutable joint Histogram of Float32 counts.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty Sources array, an invalid or mismatched source, an invalid source position or channel, a dimension count outside 1 .. 10, invalid bounds, an excessive bin product, a native int limit, a nonfinite or out-of-range Float32 sample, or a native failure.

Mask is a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 Mat with Source's rows and columns and a row stride that fits int. Source and Mask are not modified; masked-out Float32 samples are not inspected.

Parameters

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 selection with Source's rows and columns; nonzero samples select pixels.

Dimensions

Nonuniform axes in dimension order (any index bounds); every source position must be 0.

Return Value

Fresh immutable Histogram of the selected pixels' counts.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for any condition of the unmasked overload or for a Mask that is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1, of different geometry, or with an excessive row stride.

Calculate_Nonuniform_Histogram

function Calculate_Nonuniform_Histogram
  (Source     : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Dimensions : Histogram_Nonuniform_Dimension_Array) return Histogram

Dense nonuniform histogram of one source. Each axis supplies its own strictly increasing edges; bin I is [edge I, edge I + 1). Every stored source position must be 0. Otherwise as the uniform single-source overload: nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 Source, 1 .. 10 axes, channels below Source.Channels, a bin product of at most 2_147_483_647, the same Source size and stride limits, and fresh Float32 counts published only after success. Source is not modified; a Region is its own image.

The result has Nonuniform_Binning and keeps each axis's exact edges. Float32 samples are compared directly with the edges and must be finite. For UInt8 sources every edge must lie strictly between -2_147_483_647.0 and 2_147_483_647.0. A request that can reach OpenCV's IPP path (one axis on channel 0 and no mask) also needs its first edge in that interval and, for a one-channel Source, a byte row stride that fits int.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 image to sample.

Dimensions

Nonuniform axes in dimension order (any index bounds); every source position must be 0.

Return Value

Fresh immutable Histogram of Float32 counts.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, a nonzero source position, a missing channel, a dimension count outside 1 .. 10, an excessive bin product, an edge or native int limit, a nonfinite Float32 sample, or a native failure.

Calculate_Nonuniform_Histogram

   Mask       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Dimensions : Histogram_Nonuniform_Dimension_Array) return Histogram

Masked multi-source Calculate_Nonuniform_Histogram: as the unmasked overload, counting only locations whose Mask value is nonzero. Mask is a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 Mat with the common source geometry, applied at the same (row, column) of every source. Sources and Mask are not modified; masked-out Float32 samples are not inspected.

Return Value

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for any condition of the unmasked overload or for a Mask that is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1, of different geometry, or with an excessive row stride.

Calculate_Nonuniform_Histogram

function Calculate_Nonuniform_Histogram
  (Sources    : OpenCV.Core.Mat_Array;
   Dimensions : Histogram_Nonuniform_Dimension_Array) return Histogram

Dense nonuniform joint histogram. Source_Position is the zero-based position in Mat_Array iteration order, as for uniform multi-source histograms. Every axis is nonuniform. Sources follow the uniform multi-source contract (nonempty array; nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16 or Float32 elements sharing the first element's rows, columns and depth; channel counts may differ). Edge, bin product, native int and Float32 sample rules are those of the single-source nonuniform overload. The result has Nonuniform_Binning, stores each axis's source position, channel and exact edges, and is published only after success. No source is modified; a Region is its logical view.

Canny_Edges

procedure Canny_Edges
  (Source          : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination     : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Lower_Threshold : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Upper_Threshold : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Aperture        : Canny_Aperture := Sobel_3x3;
   Gradient_Norm   : Canny_Gradient_Norm := L1_Norm)

Canny_Edges finds edges in a non-empty two-dimensional UInt8 C1 Source (native cv::Canny). Lower_Threshold and Upper_Threshold must be finite, nonnegative, and ordered lower to upper. Destination receives a UInt8 C1 edge map of Source's geometry, nonzero at edge pixels and zero elsewhere, as a direct native output: storage that already has that size and type is reused (shallow copies and a Region's parent see the result); otherwise Destination is rebound to new storage.

Parameters
Source

Single-channel UInt8 image to analyse.

Destination

Receives the edge map.

Lower_Threshold

Lower hysteresis threshold on the gradient magnitude; finite, nonnegative, and not above Upper_Threshold.

Upper_Threshold

Upper hysteresis threshold on the gradient magnitude; finite and nonnegative.

Aperture

Sobel aperture of the gradient; Sobel_3x3 by default.

Gradient_Norm

Gradient magnitude norm; L1_Norm by default.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty, non-2-D, or non-UInt8 C1 Source, a negative, nonfinite, or misordered threshold, or a failure reported by OpenCV.

Canny_Gradient_Norm

type Canny_Gradient_Norm is (L1_Norm, L2_Norm);

Norm Canny_Edges uses for the gradient magnitude it thresholds.

Enumeration Literal
L1_Norm

Sum of the absolute X and Y derivatives (the default).

L2_Norm

Euclidean magnitude: square root of the sum of the squared X and Y derivatives.

CLAHE

procedure CLAHE
  (Source         : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination    : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Clip_Limit     : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 40.0;
   Tile_Grid_Size : OpenCV.Size := (Width => 8, Height => 8))

CLAHE performs Contrast Limited Adaptive Histogram Equalization locally, unlike Equalize_Histogram, which uses one global histogram. Source is divided into Tile_Grid_Size.Width by Tile_Grid_Size.Height contextual regions; Clip_Limit controls contrast limiting in each region. Source must be a non-empty two-dimensional UInt8 or UInt16 single-channel Mat. No implicit color or depth conversion is performed. Tile counts need not divide Source dimensions evenly: OpenCV pads internally as needed, using Reflect_101. Clip_Limit must be finite and greater than zero; both tile counts must be positive. Destination may initially be empty or incompatible; on success it has Source's geometry, depth, and one channel. Direct same-object in-place use, including an ROI, is supported. Distinct Source/Destination Mats with overlapping storage are rejected before native writes. Contract violations and native failures raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error.

When Source is Destination itself or a non-continuous Region, CLAHE runs on a private snapshot, so padding uses only that view's pixels, and the result is then copied into Destination. Otherwise OpenCV writes Destination directly and reads Source in place; a continuous Region (for example full-width rows) then remains a view of its parent, and padding of a size not divisible by the tile counts can read parent pixels just outside the Region. In both cases Destination's storage is reused when it already is a Mat of Source's size and type (shallow copies of Destination and a Region's parent then see the result); otherwise Destination is rebound to new storage. When storage is independent, Source remains unchanged. Tile counts, the padded geometry and the tile area must fit native int arithmetic, and Clip_Limit must not exceed 2147483647.0 * 65536.0 / Tile_Area, where Tile_Area is the pixel count of one padded tile.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 or UInt16 C1 image to equalize.

Destination

Receives the equalized image with Source's geometry and depth; may be Source itself but must not otherwise share storage with Source.

Clip_Limit

Contrast limit applied to each tile histogram; finite and greater than zero. Defaults to 40.0.

Tile_Grid_Size

Number of tiles across (Width) and down (Height), not pixel dimensions; both positive. Defaults to 8 x 8.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty, non-2-D, multi-channel or unsupported-depth Source, a nonpositive or nonfinite Clip_Limit, a zero tile count, native int limits, a Destination that shares storage with a distinct Source, or a native failure.

Clone_GrabCut_State

function Clone_GrabCut_State (State : GrabCut_State) return GrabCut_State

Explicit independent copy of an initialized state: the mask and both models are deep-copied, so refining either state never affects the other.

Raised when State is not initialized.

Color_Conversion

type Color_Conversion is
  (BGR_To_Gray,
   RGB_To_Gray,
   BGRA_To_Gray,
   RGBA_To_Gray,
   Gray_To_BGR,
   Gray_To_RGB,
   Gray_To_BGRA,
   Gray_To_RGBA,
   …,
   Premultiplied_RGBA_To_RGBA);

Selects the per-pixel color conversion performed by Convert_Color. Each name gives the Source layout, then the Destination layout, and each unpacked layout stores its channels in the order its name spells: BGR is B, G, R; BGRA is B, G, R, alpha; XYZ is X, Y, Z; YCrCb is Y, Cr, Cb; YUV is Y, U, V; HSV is H, S, V; HLS is H, L, S; Lab is L, a, b; Luv is L, u, v. OpenCV's default order is BGR, not RGB. Gray is C1, BGR565 and BGR555 are packed C2, BGRA and RGBA are C4, and every other layout is C3. Destination keeps Source rows, columns and depth.

Source depths: layout, Gray, XYZ, YCrCb and YUV selectors accept UInt8, UInt16 or Float32; HSV, HLS, Lab and Luv selectors, including the FULL and linear-light ones, accept UInt8 or Float32; packed BGR565/BGR555 and premultiplied selectors accept UInt8 only.

Gray is OpenCV's weighted luminance (UInt8 pure blue gives 29, pure red 76). Appended alpha is the depth maximum: 255 (UInt8), 65535 (UInt16) or 1.0 (Float32). Removing alpha discards it; BGRA/RGBA swaps keep it. YUV here is per-pixel C3 YUV, not a subsampled frame; see Decode_YUV420 and Decode_YUV422. Standard (non-FULL) UInt8 HSV/HLS hue is degrees / 2 (red 0, green 60, blue 120) and S, V and L use 0..255; Float32 hue is degrees. Ordinary Lab/Luv selectors apply sRGB transfer decoding (forward) and encoding (reverse). Float32 BGR/RGB is normally 0..1: normalize it before Float32 Lab/Luv. Lab/Luv lightness is 0..100 for Float32 and encoded as 0..255 for UInt8. No pixel value is range-checked or sanitized.

Packed conversions require UInt8. BGR565/BGR555 use UInt8 C2, OpenCV's native Mat representation: two bytes per native 16-bit word. Byte order is host-native, not a network/file-format byte order. BGR565 holds blue in bits 0..4, green in 5..10 and red in 11..15; BGR555 holds blue in 0..4, green in 5..9, red in 10..14 and an alpha flag in bit 15. RGB/RGBA selectors interpret channel order, not a new packed layout. Packing truncates; unpacking does not replicate low color bits, so unpacked blue and red reach at most 248 and green 252 (BGR565) or 248 (BGR555). BGR565 ignores input alpha and expands alpha to 255. BGR555 uses bit 15 as nonzero-alpha flag; C3 and Gray packing leave it zero.

FULL HSV/HLS and linear-light Lab/Luv require UInt8/Float32 C3. UInt8 FULL hue has full-byte encoding (0..255 for the whole circle); native forward scale is 256, reverse scale 255, so exact round trips are not promised. Float32 standard/FULL hue remains degrees and both paths are equivalent in reviewed OpenCV 4.1/4.10/5.0 implementations. Linear Lab/Luv omits sRGB transfer encoding/decoding, unlike ordinary Lab/Luv selectors. Float32 RGB is normally 0..1; no pixel sanitizer or manual Lab/Luv rescaling is applied. Native UInt8 encoding is retained. Premultiplied selectors require UInt8 C4 in named RGBA order, preserve alpha, and multiply color by alpha/255. Reverse saturates even for noncanonical color > alpha; zero alpha yields zero color. Quantization makes the inverse lossy, especially at low alpha.

Enumeration Literal

BGR C3 to Gray C1 luminance; UInt8/UInt16/Float32.

Lab_To_Linear_RGB

Lab C3 to linear-light RGB C3, without sRGB encoding; UInt8/Float32.

Linear_BGR_To_Luv

Linear-light BGR C3 to Luv C3, without sRGB decoding; UInt8/Float32.

Linear_RGB_To_Luv

Linear-light RGB C3 to Luv C3, without sRGB decoding; UInt8/Float32.

Luv_To_Linear_BGR

Luv C3 to linear-light BGR C3, without sRGB encoding; UInt8/Float32.

Luv_To_Linear_RGB

Luv C3 to linear-light RGB C3, without sRGB encoding; UInt8/Float32.

RGBA_To_Premultiplied_RGBA

RGBA C4 to premultiplied RGBA C4; each of R, G and B becomes (C * A + 128) / 255 (native scalar integer form) and alpha A is kept; UInt8 only.

Premultiplied_RGBA_To_RGBA

Premultiplied RGBA C4 to RGBA C4; each of R, G and B becomes the saturated integer (C * 255 + A / 2) / A, or 0 when A is 0, and alpha A is kept; UInt8 only.

Compare_Histograms

function Compare_Histograms
  (Left   : Histogram;
   Right  : Histogram;
   Method : Histogram_Comparison_Method) return OpenCV.Float64_Value

Uniform histograms must share dimension count and, per dimension, bin count and both range endpoints. Nonuniform histograms must both be nonuniform and share dimension count, bin count, and the exact stored Float32 edge sequence of every axis; matching only the first and last edges is not enough. Uniform and nonuniform histograms are never compared, even when a nonuniform sequence happens to be equal-width. Source positions and channels may differ: comparison is of bin geometry and counts, not of sample provenance. Both histograms must be calculated: a default-initialized Histogram is rejected. Neither histogram is modified.

Parameters
Left

First histogram; the reference of the asymmetric methods (the Chi_Square denominator and the Kullback_Leibler_Divergence weights).

Right

Second histogram, with Left's binning mode and bin geometry.

Method

Comparison metric; see Histogram_Comparison_Method.

Return Value

The native Float64 metric value.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for a default-initialized histogram, different binning modes, dimension counts, bin counts or edges, or a native failure.

Component_Count

function Component_Count (Self : Connected_Component_Set) return Natural

Returns the foreground count, excluding Background_Label.

Parameters
Self

Statistics set to inspect.

Return Value

Number of foreground components, zero when there is none. Valid Get_Component labels are 1 .. Component_Count.

Component_Label

type Component_Label is new Natural;

Pixel value of the Labels image of Connected_Components_With_Stats: Background_Label for background pixels and 1 .. Component_Count for the foreground components. Get_Component accepts foreground labels.

Component_Statistics

type Component_Statistics is record
   Bounds     : OpenCV.Rect;
   Area       : Natural;
   Centroid_X : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Centroid_Y : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
end record;

Statistics of one foreground component, in the coordinates of the labeled Source (relative to it when Source is a Region).

Record fields
Bounds

Smallest axis-aligned rectangle containing every pixel of the component: X and Y are its first column and row, Width and Height its extents in pixels.

Area

Number of pixels in the component.

Centroid_X

Mean column (X) of the component's pixels.

Centroid_Y

Mean row (Y) of the component's pixels.

Connected_Component_Set

type Connected_Component_Set is private;

Ada-owned foreground statistics from Connected_Components_With_Stats, indexed by Component_Label 1 .. Component_Count; the background has no entry. The set is copied out of OpenCV's statistics, so it does not depend on native storage; assignment copies it. A default-initialized set is empty.

Connected_Components_With_Stats

procedure Connected_Components_With_Stats
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Labels       : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Components   : out Connected_Component_Set;
   Connectivity : Pixel_Connectivity := Eight_Connected)

Labels the connected nonzero regions of a binary mask with cv::connectedComponentsWithStats and collects per-component statistics. Zero is background and every nonzero value is foreground. Zero in Labels is Background_Label and foreground labels are 1 through Component_Count. Foreground label numbering has no spatial-order guarantee. Labels is a direct native output: OpenCV reuses its storage when it already is an Int32 C1 Mat with Source's rows and columns (shallow copies of Labels and a Region's parent then see the labels) and otherwise rebinds it to new storage. The operation is not in place: Source and Labels must not share storage.

Parameters
Source

Binary mask: a nonempty two-dimensional UInt8 C1 Mat.

Labels

Receives the Int32 C1 label image with Source's rows and columns. Must not share storage with Source, including the same Mat, a shallow alias or an overlapping Region; this is checked before any native write.

Components

Receives the Ada-owned statistics of the foreground components only; assigned only after success.

Connectivity

Four_Connected or Eight_Connected select 4- and 8-neighborhoods; defaults to Eight_Connected.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not two-dimensional, not UInt8 or not single-channel, when Labels shares storage with Source, when OpenCV returns an invalid label count, and when OpenCV fails.

Contour

subtype Contour is OpenCV.Point_Array;

The boundary points of one shape, as a subtype of the shared point array. Any OpenCV.Point_Array, with any Natural bounds, is a Contour, and an extracted Contour can be passed directly to Draw_Polyline or Fill_Polygon. Get_Contour returns zero-based arrays; a contour without points has the null range 1 .. 0.

Contour_Approximation_Mode

type Contour_Approximation_Mode is
  (Every_Point, Simple, Teh_Chin_L1, Teh_Chin_KCOS);

How Find_Contours stores the points of each boundary. Each literal selects the OpenCV chain approximation method named below.

Enumeration Literal
Every_Point

Every boundary point is kept (OpenCV CHAIN_APPROX_NONE); consecutive points are horizontal, vertical or diagonal neighbors. A filled 4 x 4 square yields 12 points.

Simple

Horizontal, vertical and diagonal runs are compressed to their end points (OpenCV CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE), so an upright rectangle yields its four corners. The Find_Contours default.

Teh_Chin_L1

Teh-Chin chain approximation, OpenCV's CHAIN_APPROX_TC89_L1 flavor.

Teh_Chin_KCOS

Teh-Chin chain approximation, OpenCV's CHAIN_APPROX_TC89_KCOS flavor.

Contour_Count

function Contour_Count (Self : Contour_Set) return Natural

Returns the number of contours stored in Self.

Parameters
Self

Contour set to inspect.

Return Value

Number of stored contours, zero for an empty set. Valid indices are 0 .. Contour_Count (Self) - 1.

Contour_Hierarchy_Entry

type Contour_Hierarchy_Entry is record
   Next        : Optional_Contour_Index;
   Previous    : Optional_Contour_Index;
   First_Child : Optional_Contour_Index;
   Parent      : Optional_Contour_Index;
end record;

Topology of one contour in a Contour_Set, as reported by OpenCV for the Contour_Retrieval_Mode used by Find_Contours. A link is absent when there is no such contour; a present link is a Contour_Index of the same set.

Record fields
Next

Next contour at the same hierarchy level.

Previous

Previous contour at the same hierarchy level.

First_Child

First contour one level below this one, nested inside it.

Parent

Contour one level above this one, enclosing it.

Contour_Index

type Contour_Index is new Natural;

Zero-based position of a contour in a Contour_Set. Valid values are 0 .. Contour_Count - 1; hierarchy links use the same numbering.

Contour_Retrieval_Mode

type Contour_Retrieval_Mode is
  (External_Only, Flat_List, Two_Level, Full_Tree);

Which contours Find_Contours retrieves and how their hierarchy entries are linked. Each literal selects the OpenCV retrieval mode named below.

Enumeration Literal
External_Only

Only the extreme outer contours (OpenCV RETR_EXTERNAL); First_Child and Parent are absent for every contour. The Find_Contours default.

Flat_List

Every contour without hierarchical relationships (OpenCV RETR_LIST): First_Child and Parent are always absent.

Two_Level

Every contour in a two-level hierarchy (OpenCV RETR_CCOMP): external boundaries of components at the top level and boundaries of their holes at the second level. A contour inside a hole is again placed at the top level.

Full_Tree

Every contour with the full hierarchy of nested contours (OpenCV RETR_TREE); for example an outer boundary, a hole inside it and an island inside the hole form parent, child and grandchild.

Contour_Set

type Contour_Set is private;

Ada-owned result of Find_Contours: the points and hierarchy entry of every contour, indexed by Contour_Index. Points are copied as signed 32-bit coordinates, so the set does not depend on any native OpenCV result; assignment copies it. A default-initialized set is empty.

Convert_Color

procedure Convert_Color
  (Source      : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Conversion  : Color_Conversion)

Converts every pixel of Source between the color layouts named by Conversion with native cv::cvtColor. Nonempty 2-D Source, with exactly the channels named by Conversion. Layout, Gray, XYZ, YCrCb and YUV accept UInt8/UInt16/Float32; HSV, HLS, Lab and Luv (standard, FULL and linear-light) accept UInt8/Float32; BGR565/BGR555 and premultiplied selectors accept UInt8 only. Destination retains Source geometry/depth; its channel count is fixed by Conversion. Pixel values are not range-checked or normalized.

Source is read in place and Regions are logical images: only a Region's own pixels participate. The result is built in private native storage, and Destination is rebound to it only on success; on failure Destination is unchanged. The previous Destination storage, its shallow copies and a Region's parent are never written. Destination may be Source itself or share Source's storage.

Parameters
Source

Image to convert, with exactly the channel count and an accepted depth of Conversion's source layout.

Destination

Receives the result: a fresh owning Mat with Source's rows, columns and depth and the channel count of Conversion's destination layout. It may be Source.

Conversion

Source and destination layouts; see Color_Conversion.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty or not 2-D, has a channel count other than Conversion's source layout, or has a depth Conversion does not accept; when Rows * Columns, or for HSV, HLS, Lab and Luv selectors Columns * 3, exceeds 2_147_483_647; when a row stride exceeds the limit of a reachable optional native backend (IPP, Carotene or FastCV); or when OpenCV fails.

Convert_Remap_To_Fixed

function Convert_Remap_To_Fixed
  (Map_X, Map_Y : OpenCV.Core.Mat; Nearest_Neighbor_Only : Boolean := False)
   return Fixed_Remap_Maps

Converts separate absolute Float32 maps to native fixed-point maps (cv::convertMaps to Int16 C2) for the fixed-map Remap. Map_X and Map_Y must be nonempty 2-D Float32 C1 Mats of identical geometry with dimensions < 32767, and their coordinates must satisfy the rounding limits of the separate-map Remap (the Nearest_Neighbor limit when Nearest_Neighbor_Only). Conversion to fixed maps quantizes fractions to 1/32 pixel; nearest-only conversion rounds to integer coordinates and discards fractions. Int16 coordinate saturation can lose information. Inputs are unchanged.

Parameters
Map_X

Source column (X) coordinates.

Map_Y

Source row (Y) coordinates.

Nearest_Neighbor_Only

True omits interpolation coefficients, producing a nearest-only map; default False.

Return Value

Fresh owning fixed maps with the input geometry.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Invalid map type or geometry, unsafe coordinate, or an OpenCV failure.

Convert_Remap_To_Fixed

function Convert_Remap_To_Fixed
  (Map_XY : OpenCV.Core.Mat; Nearest_Neighbor_Only : Boolean := False)
   return Fixed_Remap_Maps

Converts one absolute Float32 C2 map (component 0 = X, component 1 = Y) to native fixed-point maps. Map_XY must be nonempty and 2-D with dimensions < 32767. Coordinate limits, 1/32-pixel quantization, nearest-only rounding and Int16 saturation are as for the separate-map overload. Map_XY is unchanged.

Parameters
Map_XY

Source (X, Y) coordinates.

Nearest_Neighbor_Only

True omits interpolation coefficients, producing a nearest-only map; default False.

Return Value

Fresh owning fixed maps with Map_XY's geometry.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Invalid map type or geometry, unsafe coordinate, or an OpenCV failure.

Convert_Remap_To_Interleaved_Float

function Convert_Remap_To_Interleaved_Float

Reconstructs one absolute Float32 C2 map (component 0 = X, component 1 = Y) from fixed maps with native cv::convertMaps. Coefficient-bearing maps yield coordinates quantized to 1/32 pixel; nearest-only maps yield their already-rounded integer coordinates. Maps must not be empty.
