Corner_Aperture

type Corner_Aperture is (Aperture_3, Aperture_5, Aperture_7);

Sobel derivative aperture of the corner response maps, passed to OpenCV as aperture size 3, 5 or 7. Pre-corner detection needs second derivatives, so the Sobel Kernel_1 selector is not applicable.

Enumeration Literal
Aperture_3

3x3 Sobel aperture, the default of every corner map.

Aperture_5

5x5 Sobel aperture.

Aperture_7

7x7 Sobel aperture.

Corner_Block_Size

subtype Corner_Block_Size is Positive range 1 .. 2_147_483_647;

Side length, in pixels, of the square neighborhood over which the corner maps accumulate the derivative covariance (native box filter size). The subtype admits 1, but every operation taking it requires at least 2 and raises OpenCV_Error for 1.

Corner_Dead_Zone

type Corner_Dead_Zone (Enabled : Boolean := False) is record
   case Enabled is
      when False =>
         null;
      when True =>
         Half_Size : OpenCV.Size;
   end case;
end record;

Optional central dead zone of the Refine_Corners_Subpixel search window, given as a half-size and passed to native cornerSubPix as its zeroZone. When disabled (the default) the native no-zone value (-1, -1) is passed.

Record fields
Enabled

Discriminant selecting whether a dead zone is used; default False.

Half_Size

Exists only when Enabled is True: dead-zone half width and half height in pixels. Each must be smaller than the matching Search_Window half-size; (0, 0) is accepted.

Corner_Eigenvalues_And_Vectors

function Corner_Eigenvalues_And_Vectors
  (Source     : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Block_Size : Corner_Block_Size := 3;
   Aperture   : Corner_Aperture := Aperture_3;
   Border     : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Reflect_101)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Per-pixel eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the Block_Size covariance matrix of Sobel derivatives (native cv::cornerEigenValsAndVecs). Six channels per pixel: (lambda1, lambda2, x1, y1, x2, y2). Source requirements, snapshot and Region isolation, border support and native limits are those of Corner_Minimum_Eigenvalue.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 or Float32 C1 image to analyse.

Block_Size

Covariance neighborhood side length; at least 2. Default 3.

Aperture

Sobel derivative aperture; default Aperture_3.

Border

Constant_Border, Replicate, Reflect or Reflect_101 (the default); Wrap is rejected.

Return Value

Fresh owning Float32 C6 Mat with Source's rows and columns, channels in the OpenCV order above.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Unsupported Source type or layout, Block_Size 1, Wrap border, native size limits, or an OpenCV failure.

Corner_Iteration_Limit

subtype Corner_Iteration_Limit is Positive range 1 .. 100;

Maximum number of Refine_Corners_Subpixel iterations, 1 through 100.

Corner_Minimum_Eigenvalue

function Corner_Minimum_Eigenvalue
  (Source     : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Block_Size : Corner_Block_Size := 3;
   Aperture   : Corner_Aperture := Aperture_3;
   Border     : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Reflect_101)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Minimum-eigenvalue corner response map (native cv::cornerMinEigenVal): per pixel, the smaller eigenvalue of the covariance matrix of Sobel derivatives accumulated over a Block_Size by Block_Size neighborhood. Source must be a nonempty 2-D UInt8 or Float32 C1 Mat. It is copied into a packed private snapshot before native execution, so Source is unchanged and a Region is an independent image whose borders never read parent pixels. Native signed-int layout limits on the Source size and Block_Size raise OpenCV_Error.

Parameters
Source

Image to analyse.

Block_Size

Covariance neighborhood side length; at least 2. Default 3.

Aperture

Sobel derivative aperture; default Aperture_3.

Border

Constant_Border, Replicate, Reflect or Reflect_101 (the default); Wrap is rejected.

Return Value

Fresh owning Float32 C1 Mat with Source's rows and columns.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Unsupported Source type or layout, Block_Size 1, Wrap border, native size limits, or an OpenCV failure.

Corner_Point_Array

type Corner_Point_Array is array (Integer range <>) of OpenCV.Float32_Point;

Float32 corner positions for Refine_Corners_Subpixel, in pixels (X = column, Y = row). Any Integer index range is allowed; refined results keep the bounds of the input array.

Corner_Termination

type Corner_Termination is record
   Maximum_Iterations : Corner_Iteration_Limit := 30;
   Epsilon            : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 0.01;
end record;

Termination criteria of Refine_Corners_Subpixel. Both criteria are always passed to native cornerSubPix (MAX_ITER and EPS), so refinement stops at Maximum_Iterations or earlier once the native Epsilon accuracy criterion is met.

Record fields
Maximum_Iterations

Iteration limit; default 30.

Epsilon

Native EPS accuracy threshold; default 0.01. Refine_Corners_Subpixel requires it finite and nonnegative.

Create_Hanning_Window

function Create_Hanning_Window
  (Window_Size : OpenCV.Size;
   Depth       : Hanning_Window_Depth := Float64_Hanning_Window)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Creates a two-dimensional Hann window with native cv::createHanningWindow, for example as a Phase_Correlate Window. Each coefficient is the square root of the separable product of a horizontal and a vertical Hann coefficient, with native rounding. The window is symmetric, its first and last rows and columns are zero up to rounding, and an odd-sized window is approximately 1.0 at its center. OpenCV 5.0 vector cosine paths can round differently from 4.1 and 4.10; bit-for-bit coefficients are not promised.

Parameters
Window_Size

Window columns (Width) and rows (Height). Both must exceed one, and Width * Height must not exceed 2**31 - 1.

Depth

Coefficient depth; defaults to Float64_Hanning_Window.

Return Value

Fresh owning C1 Mat of the requested depth with Window_Size.Height rows and Window_Size.Width columns.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

A dimension is at most one, the allocation or index preflight overflows, or OpenCV fails.

Decode_YUV420

function Decode_YUV420
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Layout : YUV420_Layout;
   Output : YUV420_Color_Output := BGR_Output) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Decodes a packed YUV 4:2:0 frame to UInt8 color with native OpenCV. Packed sources/results are UInt8 C1, W columns and H+H/2 rows. Logical W/H are positive even values, at least 2, so Source has a row count that is a multiple of 3 and H is two thirds of it. I420 is Y,U,V; YV12 is Y,V,U; NV12 is Y then U,V pairs; NV21 is Y then V,U pairs.

All YUV 4:2:0 operations take packed independent snapshots, leave inputs unchanged and return fresh owning Mats. Violations raise OpenCV_Error. A packed Region must itself contain a complete standalone frame: parent plane offsets and ROI origins are never inferred. Packed NV12/NV21 frames are split into Y and UV planes and decoded by the same native cvtColorTwoPlane route as Decode_YUV420_Two_Plane, so both representations of a frame decode to identical pixels within one native build.

Native BT.601 limited-range conversion (nominal Y 16..235 and U/V 16..240, centered on 128) accepts all byte values: Y - 16 is clamped at zero and RGB saturates. Vendor rounding can differ from the portable 20-bit CPU arithmetic. Decode_YUV420 returns UInt8 H x W C3 BGR/RGB or C4 BGRA/RGBA, with alpha 255.

Parameters
Source

Packed frame: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 with an even column count of at least 2 and a row count that is a positive multiple of 3.

Layout

Plane order of Source.

Output

Channel layout of the result; BGR_Output by default.

Return Value

Fresh owning UInt8 Mat of H rows and W columns: C3 B,G,R or R,G,B, or C4 B,G,R,255 or R,G,B,255, as selected by Output.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D or not UInt8 C1; when its column count is odd or below 2 or its row count is not a positive multiple of 3; when a native signed-arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded (for example W * H above 2_147_483_647); or when OpenCV fails.

Decode_YUV420_Two_Plane

function Decode_YUV420_Two_Plane
  (Y_Plane  : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   UV_Plane : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Layout   : YUV420_Semiplanar_Layout;
   Output   : YUV420_Color_Output := BGR_Output) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Decodes a semiplanar YUV 4:2:0 frame supplied as separate planes, with native cvtColorTwoPlane. Y: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 H x W. UV: UInt8 C2 H/2 x W/2. Caller supplies corresponding planes/Regions; no chroma phase is inferred. Independent packed snapshots have equal byte step W, normalizing OpenCV 4.1's shared-stride assumption. Both semiplanar decode forms use cvtColorTwoPlane, with exact representation equality: within one native build the result equals Decode_YUV420 of the same frame packed.

Inputs stay unchanged and only the logical pixels of Regions participate. Conversion is the native BT.601 limited-range arithmetic described for Decode_YUV420: all byte values are accepted and vendor rounding can differ from the portable 20-bit CPU arithmetic.

Parameters
Y_Plane

Luma plane: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 of H rows and W columns, with W and H even and at least 2.

UV_Plane

Interleaved chroma plane: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C2 of exactly H/2 rows and W/2 columns; each element is the U,V (NV12) or V,U (NV21) pair of one 2x2 pixel block.

Layout

Element order of UV_Plane: NV12 or NV21.

Output

Channel layout of the result; BGR_Output by default.

Return Value

Fresh owning UInt8 Mat of H rows and W columns: C3 B,G,R or R,G,B, or C4 B,G,R,255 or R,G,B,255, as selected by Output.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when a plane is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 or has the wrong channel count; when W or H is odd or below 2; when UV_Plane is not exactly H/2 x W/2; when a native signed-arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded (for example W * H above 2_147_483_647); or when OpenCV fails.

Decode_YUV422

function Decode_YUV422
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Layout : YUV422_Layout;
   Output : YUV422_Color_Output := BGR_Output) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Decodes a packed YUV 4:2:2 frame to UInt8 color with native cv::cvtColor. Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C2, H >=1, even W >=2. Each C2 element is two stream bytes: UYVY = U,Y0,V,Y1; YUY2 = Y0,U,Y1,V; YVYU = Y0,V,Y1,U. Each U/V pair serves two horizontal pixels. Results are fresh owning H x W UInt8 Mats; Source stays unchanged. Independent logical-row snapshots treat Regions as standalone frames. Caller supplies the declared byte phase: odd-column parent ROIs may change it; Layout is never inferred from the parent's ROI origin. Invalid sources/native arithmetic spans raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error. OpenCV 4.1 lacks the native even-width check; Ada and the shim enforce it before pair reads. Decode uses native limited-range BT.601 arithmetic (nominal Y 16..235 and U/V 16..240, centered on 128), accepting all UInt8 values with native saturation. Vendor/build pixel identity is not promised. Decode_YUV422 returns C3 BGR/RGB or C4 BGRA/RGBA, alpha 255; geometry is preserved.

Parameters
Source

Packed frame: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C2 with at least one row and an even column count of at least 2.

Layout

Byte order of Source.

Output

Channel layout of the result; BGR_Output by default.

Return Value

Fresh owning UInt8 Mat with Source's rows and columns: C3 B,G,R or R,G,B, or C4 B,G,R,255 or R,G,B,255, as selected by Output.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D or not UInt8 C2; when its column count is odd or below 2; when 2 * W or W * H exceeds 2_147_483_647 or another native arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded; or when OpenCV fails.

Default_Morphology_Border

Default_Morphology_Border : constant Morphology_Border_Value;

OpenCV's special neutral border, not zero: outside pixels act as the maximum for erosion and the minimum for dilation, so they never affect the result. Valid for every Source depth and channel count.

Demosaic_Bayer

function Demosaic_Bayer
  (Source  : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Pattern : Bayer_Pattern;
   Method  : Bayer_Demosaicing_Method := Bilinear;
   Order   : Bayer_Color_Order := BGR_Order) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Demosaics a single-channel Bayer mosaic to color with native cv::demosaicing. Pattern describes the physical 2x2 CFA at logical Source (0,0). Cropping does not automatically adjust the parent's Bayer phase. Every Bayer operation requires nonempty 2-D C1 UInt8/UInt16, at least 3x3. VNG requires UInt8. No depth conversion is performed.

Source is unchanged; an independent packed snapshot isolates Regions from parent pixels. Results are fresh owning Mats of the same size and depth. Invalid sources raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error.

For Variable_Number_Of_Gradients, OpenCV falls back to bilinear demosaicing when either dimension is below 8. In OpenCV 4.1/4.10 that fallback decodes every Pattern and Order as BGGR/BGR; 5.0 honors them, so select Bilinear for correct phase and order on small images across releases. OpenCV 5 VNG uses two-pixel reflected (Reflect_101) padding; cross-version VNG border values are not promised identical. The installed algorithm is preserved. Demosaic_Bayer returns C3 BGR or RGB according to Order. Legacy edge-aware interpolation (including OpenCV 4.1) can round diagonal color averages differently for the BGR and RGB selectors. Channel order is preserved, but reversing channels need not produce bit-identical samples; native selector results are retained.

Parameters
Source

Bayer mosaic: nonempty 2-D C1 UInt8 or UInt16 of at least 3 rows and 3 columns; UInt8 for Variable_Number_Of_Gradients.

Pattern

CFA tile at logical Source (0,0).

Method

Interpolation algorithm; Bilinear by default.

Order

Channel order of the result; BGR_Order by default.

Return Value

Fresh owning C3 Mat with Source's rows, columns and depth, in B,G,R or R,G,B order as selected by Order.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not C1, smaller than 3x3, not UInt8 or UInt16, or UInt16 with Variable_Number_Of_Gradients; when a native signed-arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded; or when OpenCV fails.

Demosaic_Bayer_To_Gray

function Demosaic_Bayer_To_Gray
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat; Pattern : Bayer_Pattern)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Direct native Bayer luminance, C1 (not color followed by conversion), interpolated bilinearly at Pattern's phase. In a uniform area of the mosaic the value is the native fixed-point (4899 * R + 9617 * G + 1868 * B + 8192) / 16384; UInt8 SIMD rounding can differ by one.

Source requirements, snapshot isolation and Pattern phase are as for Demosaic_Bayer: nonempty 2-D C1 UInt8/UInt16 of at least 3x3, copied into an independent packed snapshot, with Pattern at logical (0,0). On OpenCV 4.x before 4.5.5, a UInt16 Source is rejected when the four green neighbors of any interior red or blue site sum above 223300, which would overflow native signed arithmetic; later releases have no such restriction.

Parameters
Source

Bayer mosaic: nonempty 2-D C1 UInt8 or UInt16 of at least 3 rows and 3 columns.

Pattern

CFA tile at logical Source (0,0).

Return Value

Fresh owning C1 Mat with Source's rows, columns and depth.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not C1, smaller than 3x3, or not UInt8 or UInt16; when the legacy UInt16 neighbor limit above applies; when a native signed-arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded; or when OpenCV fails.

Demosaic_Bayer_With_Alpha

function Demosaic_Bayer_With_Alpha
  (Source  : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Pattern : Bayer_Pattern;
   Order   : Bayer_Color_Order := BGR_Order) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Bilinear C4 BGRA/RGBA; native alpha is 255/65535 for UInt8/UInt16. Source requirements, snapshot isolation and Pattern phase are as for Demosaic_Bayer: nonempty 2-D C1 UInt8/UInt16 of at least 3x3, copied into an independent packed snapshot, with Pattern at logical (0,0).

Parameters
Source

Bayer mosaic: nonempty 2-D C1 UInt8 or UInt16 of at least 3 rows and 3 columns.

Pattern

CFA tile at logical Source (0,0).

Order

Color channel order of the result; BGR_Order by default.

Return Value

Fresh owning C4 Mat with Source's rows, columns and depth, in B,G,R,alpha or R,G,B,alpha order as selected by Order.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not C1, smaller than 3x3, or not UInt8 or UInt16; when a native signed-arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded; or when OpenCV fails.

Derivative_Axis

type Derivative_Axis is (X_Axis, Y_Axis);

Axis of the first derivative computed by Scharr and Get_Scharr_Kernels.

Enumeration Literal
X_Axis

Horizontal derivative: X order 1, Y order 0.

Y_Axis

Vertical derivative: X order 0, Y order 1.

Derivative_Depth

type Derivative_Depth is
  (Same_Depth, Int16_Depth, Float32_Depth, Float64_Depth);

Destination depth of Sobel, Scharr and Laplacian, and through Filter_Depth of Filter_2D and Sep_Filter_2D. Supported per Source depth: UInt8 accepts every literal; UInt16 and Int16 accept Same_Depth, Float32_Depth and Float64_Depth; Float32 accepts Same_Depth or Float32_Depth; Float64 accepts Same_Depth or Float64_Depth. Other combinations raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error.

Enumeration Literal
Same_Depth

Destination keeps Source's depth.

Int16_Depth

Destination is Int16 (UInt8 Sources only).

Float32_Depth

Destination is Float32.

Float64_Depth

Destination is Float64.

Derivative_Kernel_Depth

subtype Derivative_Kernel_Depth is Gaussian_Kernel_Depth;

Coefficient depth of Get_Derivative_Kernels and Get_Scharr_Kernels, with the Gaussian_Kernel_Depth literals.

Derivative_Kernel_Normalization

type Derivative_Kernel_Normalization is (Unnormalized, Normalized);

Coefficient scaling of Get_Derivative_Kernels and Get_Scharr_Kernels. It never changes the derivative order or the vector lengths.

Enumeration Literal
Unnormalized

OpenCV's integer coefficients, such as [-1, 0, 1] with [1, 2, 1] for a first-order 3-tap Sobel pair.

Normalized

OpenCV's normalized scaling, such as [-0.5, 0, 0.5] with [0.25, 0.5, 0.25] for that pair; for Scharr only the smoothing vector is scaled.

Derivative_Kernels

type Derivative_Kernels is record
   Kernel_X : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel_Y : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
end record;

Separable coefficient pair returned by Get_Derivative_Kernels and Get_Scharr_Kernels, ready for Sep_Filter_2D. Each component is an independently owned N x 1 single-channel column vector; both have the requested depth, and their lengths may differ (Kernel_1).

Record fields
Kernel_X

Coefficients applied along X, that is within each row (Sep_Filter_2D's Kernel_X).

Kernel_Y

Coefficients applied along Y, that is within each column (Sep_Filter_2D's Kernel_Y).

Derivative_Order

subtype Derivative_Order is Natural range 0 .. 7;

Order of a spatial derivative along one axis; 0 means no derivative on that axis. Sobel and Get_Derivative_Kernels also require at least one nonzero order and each order below the effective kernel length.

Dilate

procedure Dilate
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the centered Kernel_Size Dilate, but with a caller-supplied structuring element anchored at (Kernel.Columns / 2, Kernel.Rows / 2). Kernel is a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask: zero excludes, every nonzero value includes, without weighting; all-zero masks are rejected. Even, non-square and non-contiguous Region kernels are accepted; only the kernel's logical view participates and Kernel is not modified. Kernel and Destination must not overlap; Source/Destination in-place is supported, including a parent-backed Region. Border, Destination and native-limit behavior are those of the Kernel_Size overload.

Parameters
Source

Image to dilate.

Destination

Receives the dilated image (direct native output with Source's geometry and element type).

Kernel

Structuring element: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask whose nonzero elements are included.

Iterations

Number of successive dilations; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source or Kernel, an all-zero Kernel, Kernel storage overlapping Destination, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Dilate

procedure Dilate
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Anchor       : OpenCV.Point;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the custom-Kernel Dilate, but with an explicit Anchor inside Kernel, where (0, 0) is the top-left kernel element; no negative center sentinel exists. The kernel's footprint shifts with Anchor. Kernel requirements, overlap rejection, Border, Destination, in-place, Region and native-limit behavior are those of the centered overload.

Iterations

Number of successive dilations; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source or Kernel, an all-zero Kernel, an Anchor outside Kernel, Kernel storage overlapping Destination, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Dilate

procedure Dilate
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel_Size  : OpenCV.Size;
   Shape        : Morphology_Shape := Rectangle;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

Dilate replaces Destination with the neighborhood maximum selected by a temporary structuring element of Kernel_Size and Shape, anchored at (Width / 2, Height / 2). It has the same source depth, channel, kernel-size, iteration, border, destination, in-place and native-limit semantics as Erode: Destination receives Source's geometry and element type as a direct native output, and Source may also be Destination. Constant_Border uses Border_Value, by default OpenCV's neutral morphology border value. A Region is processed as its own logical image; pixels in its parent outside the view do not participate.

Parameters
Source

Image to dilate.

Destination

Receives the dilated image.

Kernel_Size

Width (columns) and height (rows) of the generated structuring element; both must be positive.

Shape

Generated element shape; defaults to Rectangle.

Iterations

Number of successive dilations; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, a zero kernel dimension, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Dilate

procedure Dilate
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel_Size  : OpenCV.Size;
   Shape        : Morphology_Shape;
   Anchor       : OpenCV.Point;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the centered Kernel_Size Dilate, but with an explicit Anchor inside the Kernel_Size box, where (0, 0) is the top-left element pixel; no negative center sentinel exists. A generated Cross passes through Anchor; Rectangle and Ellipse masks do not depend on Anchor, but their footprint shifts with it. Source, Destination, border, in-place, Region and native-limit behavior are those of the centered overload.

Parameters

Image to dilate.

Destination

Receives the dilated image (direct native output with Source's geometry and element type).

Kernel_Size

Width (columns) and height (rows) of the generated structuring element; both must be positive.

Generated element shape; no default in this overload.

Anchor

Element position aligned with each output pixel; it must lie inside the element.

Iterations

Number of successive dilations; defaults to 1.

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Raised exceptions

Raised for an invalid Source, a zero kernel dimension, an Anchor outside the element, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Distance_Label_Mode

type Distance_Label_Mode is (Nearest_Zero_Component, Nearest_Zero_Pixel);

How Distance_Transform_With_Labels numbers the zero pixels.

Enumeration Literal
Nearest_Zero_Component

Each 8-connected component of zero pixels receives one positive label.

Nearest_Zero_Pixel

Each zero pixel receives its own positive label, assigned in row-major scan order (a discrete Voronoi map).

Distance_Transform

function Distance_Transform
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Method : Distance_Transform_Method := Euclidean_Precise)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Computes the distance from every pixel of Source to the nearest zero pixel. Source is a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 image containing at least one zero. Zero pixels are targets with distance 0; every nonzero pixel is foreground (1 and 255 behave alike). Results are fresh, source-preserving Mats of the same geometry; Regions are independent logical images whose parent pixels do not participate. L1 and chessboard distances are exact. Euclidean_3x3 and Euclidean_5x5 use OpenCV's approximate weights; Euclidean_Precise is exact up to Float32 rounding. The result is Float32 C1.

The shim rejects images whose row stride, pixel count, row offsets or padded temporary exceed native signed-int arithmetic, so a small Region of a very large parent can be rejected. Euclidean_Precise also limits each dimension to 46341 (OpenCV 4.1 table arithmetic).

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask; zero marks the targets.

Method

Metric and mask; defaults to Euclidean_Precise.

Return Value

Fresh owning Float32 C1 distance Mat with Source's rows and columns.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1 or has no zero pixel, when a native size limit is exceeded, or when OpenCV fails.

Distance_Transform_Method

type Distance_Transform_Method is
  (Manhattan_Distance,
   Chessboard_Distance,
   Euclidean_3x3,
   Euclidean_5x5,
   Euclidean_Precise);

Metric and propagation mask of Distance_Transform. Distances are in pixels, from each pixel to the nearest zero pixel.

Enumeration Literal
Manhattan_Distance

Exact L1 distance |dx| + |dy|.

Chessboard_Distance

Exact chessboard distance max (|dx|, |dy|).

Euclidean_3x3

Fast approximation with OpenCV's 3x3 weights (axis step about 0.955, diagonal step about 1.3693).

Euclidean_5x5

Approximation with OpenCV's 5x5 weights (axis step 1.0), closer to the true distance than Euclidean_3x3.

Euclidean_Precise

Exact Euclidean distance up to Float32 rounding. Each Source dimension is limited to 46341 pixels.

Distance_Transform_With_Labels

function Distance_Transform_With_Labels
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Metric : Labeled_Distance_Metric := Euclidean_Label_Distance;
   Labels : Distance_Label_Mode := Nearest_Zero_Component)
   return Labeled_Distance_Transform_Result

Computes distances to the nearest zero pixel together with the label of that zero. Source requirements, Region behavior and native size limits are those of Distance_Transform. Distances are Float32 C1, Labels Int32 C1. Component mode groups 8-connected zero pixels; pixel mode assigns row-major positive labels to individual zeros. Ties have no specified winner. Euclidean labeled distances use the native 5x5 approximation; precise is unavailable.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask containing at least one zero pixel; zero marks the labeled targets.

Metric

Distance metric; defaults to Euclidean_Label_Distance.

Labels

Labeling of the zero pixels; defaults to Nearest_Zero_Component.

Return Value

Record whose Distances and Labels are fresh owning Mats with Source's rows and columns.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8 C1 or has no zero pixel, when a native size limit is exceeded, or when OpenCV fails.

Draw_Arrow

procedure Draw_Arrow
  (Image      : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Start      : OpenCV.Point;
   Finish     : OpenCV.Point;
   Color      : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Tip_Length : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 0.1;
   Thickness  : Drawing_Thickness := 1;
   Line_Style : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line)

Draws an arrow into Image in place with cv::arrowedLine, under the package drawing rules: the shaft from Start to Finish and, at Finish, an arrowhead of two strokes at 45 degrees to the shaft, each Tip_Length times the Start-to-Finish distance long. Tip_Length is a finite fraction in (0, 1]. Coincident endpoints are rejected. Unsafe native coordinate arithmetic raises OpenCV_Error: the endpoint differences and the arrowhead coordinates must fit the signed 32-bit range.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Start

Tail of the arrow; may lie outside Image.

Finish

Tip of the arrow, where the arrowhead is drawn; must differ from Start.

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Tip_Length

Arrowhead stroke length as a fraction of the arrow length: finite, greater than 0.0 and at most 1.0. Defaults to 0.1.

Thickness

Thickness of shaft and arrowhead in pixels; defaults to 1.

Line_Style

Rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Tip_Length is outside (0, 1] or not finite, when Start equals Finish, when the native coordinate arithmetic would overflow, when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, and when OpenCV fails.

procedure Draw_Circle

Draw_Contours

procedure Draw_Contours
  (Image             : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Contours          : Contour_Set;
   Root              : Contour_Index;
   Color             : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Descendant_Levels : Natural := 0;
   Thickness         : Drawing_Thickness := 1;
   Line_Style        : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line;
   Offset            : OpenCV.Point := (X => 0, Y => 0))

Outlines the contour Root and up to Descendant_Levels generations of its descendants into Image in place; otherwise as the all-contours Draw_Contours. Contours are selected in Ada, independently of the linked OpenCV version, by following First_Child and Next links rather than storage order: zero levels means Root only, and a depth beyond the tree includes every reachable descendant. Contours without points are skipped. Offset shifts points in image/Region-local coordinates. Image and Color obey the ordinary drawing contract. Structural errors, including an invalid Root, leave Image unchanged; native drawing errors need not roll back.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Contours

Contour set containing Root, normally from Find_Contours.

Root

Index of the selected subtree's root; must be less than Contour_Count (Contours).

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Descendant_Levels

Generations below Root to include: zero selects Root only, one adds its children, two also adds grandchildren. Defaults to 0.

Thickness

Outline thickness in pixels; defaults to 1.

Line_Style

Rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Offset

Added to every point before rasterization, in Image (Region-local) coordinates; defaults to (0, 0).

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Root is not less than Contour_Count (Contours), when the hierarchy reached from Root is inconsistent, when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, when the selected point count or a point plus Offset exceeds the signed 32-bit range, and when OpenCV fails.

Draw_Contours

procedure Draw_Contours
  (Image      : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Contours   : Contour_Set;
   Color      : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Thickness  : Drawing_Thickness := 1;
   Line_Style : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line;
   Offset     : OpenCV.Point := (X => 0, Y => 0))

Outlines every contour stored in Contours into Image in place with cv::drawContours, under the package drawing rules. Select contours in Ada, independently of the linked OpenCV version: all-contours overloads use every stored contour (the Root overloads select a subtree), contours without points are skipped, and the selection is passed to OpenCV as independent contours without native hierarchy. Offset shifts points in image/Region-local coordinates. Image and Color obey the ordinary drawing contract, including UInt8-only Anti_Aliased_Line. When no selected contour has points the call is a no-op after Image, Color and Line_Style validation. Counts and point-plus-Offset sums are checked before any pixel is written: structural errors leave Image unchanged; native drawing errors need not roll back.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Contours

Contour set to draw, normally from Find_Contours.

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Thickness

Outline thickness in pixels; defaults to 1.

Line_Style

Rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Offset

Added to every point before rasterization, in Image (Region-local) coordinates; defaults to (0, 0).

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, when the selected point or contour count exceeds the signed 32-bit range, when a point plus Offset leaves that range, and when OpenCV fails.

Draw_Ellipse

procedure Draw_Ellipse
  (Image       : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Center      : OpenCV.Point;
   Axes        : OpenCV.Size;
   Angle       : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Start_Angle : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   End_Angle   : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Color       : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Thickness   : Drawing_Thickness := 1;
   Line_Style  : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line;
   Units       : OpenCV.Angle_Unit := OpenCV.Degrees)

Outlines an elliptic arc in Image in place with cv::ellipse, under the package drawing rules. The ellipse is centered on Center with semi-axes Axes and rotated by Angle; the arc runs from Start_Angle to End_Angle, so a full turn draws the closed outline. With Angle zero, Axes.Width lies along X and arc angles increase from the +X direction toward +Y (downward); an Angle of 90 degrees turns Axes.Width vertical. Axes are the ellipse semi-axis lengths and must both be positive. Angle, Start_Angle, and End_Angle must be finite. Radians are converted to degrees in Ada; values are not otherwise normalized.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Center

Ellipse center; may lie outside Image.

Axes

Semi-axis lengths in pixels, Width for the first axis and Height for the second; both must be positive.

Angle

Rotation of the ellipse, in Units; must be finite.

Start_Angle

Start of the arc, in Units; must be finite.

End_Angle

End of the arc, in Units; must be finite.

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Thickness

Outline thickness in pixels; defaults to 1.

Line_Style

Rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Units

Unit of Angle, Start_Angle and End_Angle; defaults to Degrees.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when an axis is zero, when an angle is not finite, when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, and when OpenCV fails.

Draw_Polyline

Draw_Rectangle

procedure Draw_Rectangle
  (Image      : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Bounds     : OpenCV.Rect;
   Color      : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Thickness  : Drawing_Thickness := 1;
   Line_Style : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line)

Outlines the rectangle Bounds in Image in place with cv::rectangle, under the package drawing rules. The outline runs through the border pixels of columns Bounds.X .. Bounds.X + Bounds.Width - 1 and rows Bounds.Y .. Bounds.Y + Bounds.Height - 1; the interior is not filled. Width and Height must be positive. The far corner is saturated to the signed 32-bit range, so extents reaching beyond it are clipped instead of overflowing.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Bounds

Rectangle to outline, with positive Width and Height; it may extend outside Image.

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Thickness

Outline thickness in pixels; defaults to 1.

Line_Style

Rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Bounds.Width or Bounds.Height is zero, when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, and when OpenCV fails.

Draw_Text

procedure Draw_Text
  (Image              : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Text               : String;
   Origin             : OpenCV.Point;
   Color              : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Font               : Text_Font := Hershey_Simplex;
   Font_Scale         : OpenCV.Float64_Value := 1.0;
   Thickness          : Drawing_Thickness := 1;
   Bottom_Left_Origin : Boolean := False)

Renders Text into Image in place with cv::putText. Legacy Hershey selectors: OpenCV 4.x uses strokes; OpenCV 5 uses its built-in TrueType compatibility renderer. Glyphs and metrics may differ. Requires a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1, C3 or C4 image, including a Region; this is narrower than the package drawing rules, whose color, alias and clipping rules otherwise apply. Text is a byte span, including embedded NULs, with any lower bound; how non-ASCII bytes are rendered depends on the OpenCV generation. Empty text is a no-op after the Font_Scale, image and color checks. There is no line-style choice: the binding always requests OpenCV LINE_8. Glyphs extend right of Origin and, by default, upward from it. Bottom_Left_Origin reverses the vertical direction of the glyphs.

For nonempty text, a conservative native-arithmetic preflight requires Font_Scale <= 1.0e6 / 65536.0 (about 15.26) and Text'Length * Font_Scale * 512.0 + Thickness < 1.0e6; each Origin coordinate must also stay at least (Text'Length + 1) * Font_Scale * 512.0 + Thickness + 64.0 (rounded up) inside the signed 32-bit range.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1, C3 or C4 Mat or Region.

Text

Bytes to render, passed with their exact length.

Origin

Bottom-left corner of the text in Image coordinates; may lie outside Image.

Color

Text value in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Font

Font selector; defaults to Hershey_Simplex.

Font_Scale

Font scale factor: finite, positive and within the preflight bound. Defaults to 1.0.

Thickness

Glyph stroke thickness; defaults to 1.

Bottom_Left_Origin

When True the glyphs are vertically reversed and extend downward from Origin; defaults to False.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Font_Scale is not finite and positive, when Image is not a nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1, C3 or C4 Mat, when a used Color component is not finite, when the text geometry or Origin fails the preflight, and when OpenCV fails.

Drawing_Line_Style

type Drawing_Line_Style is
  (Four_Connected_Line, Eight_Connected_Line, Anti_Aliased_Line);

Rasterization of drawn lines and shape edges, separate from Pixel_Connectivity. Every operation with a Line_Style parameter defaults to Eight_Connected_Line.

Enumeration Literal
Four_Connected_Line

4-connected lines (OpenCV LINE_4): successive pixels share an edge.

Eight_Connected_Line

8-connected lines (OpenCV LINE_8): successive pixels may also touch diagonally.

Anti_Aliased_Line

Antialiased lines (OpenCV LINE_AA) with intermediate edge intensities. Accepted only for UInt8 images; other depths raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error, where OpenCV would silently draw LINE_8 instead.

Drawing_Marker_Kind

type Drawing_Marker_Kind is
  (Cross_Marker,
   Tilted_Cross_Marker,
   Star_Marker,
   Diamond_Marker,
   Square_Marker,
   Triangle_Up_Marker,
   Triangle_Down_Marker);

Marker shape for Draw_Marker, centered on the marker Position. Each literal selects the OpenCV marker type at the same position, from MARKER_CROSS through MARKER_TRIANGLE_DOWN.

Enumeration Literal
Cross_Marker

Upright crosshair: one horizontal and one vertical stroke through Position.

Tilted_Cross_Marker

Crosshair tilted by 45 degrees: two diagonal strokes through Position.

Star_Marker

Combination of Cross_Marker and Tilted_Cross_Marker.

Diamond_Marker

Square outline rotated by 45 degrees, with vertices above, below and beside Position.

Square_Marker

Axis-aligned square outline around Position.

Triangle_Up_Marker

Triangle outline pointing up, with its apex above Position.

Triangle_Down_Marker

Triangle outline pointing down, with a horizontal top edge above Position.

Drawing_Radius

subtype Drawing_Radius is Positive;

Circle radius in pixels. Zero is excluded because OpenCV would draw a degenerate point.

Drawing_Thickness

subtype Drawing_Thickness is Positive range 1 .. 32_767;

Thickness in pixels of drawn lines and outlines, and the glyph stroke thickness of text. The upper bound keeps OpenCV's signed raster offsets in range. Fill operations take no thickness: they select OpenCV's filled mode internally, and negative thickness is not part of the API.

Earth_Mover_Bounded_Result

type Earth_Mover_Bounded_Result is record
   Distance                : OpenCV.Float32_Value;
   Lower_Bound             : OpenCV.Float32_Value;
   Lower_Bound_Available   : Boolean;
   Exact_Distance_Computed : Boolean;
end record;

Result of Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Lower_Bound. Never infer exactness from Distance = Lower_Bound: an exact distance can equal its bound.

Record fields
Distance

The exact native distance when Exact_Distance_Computed is True; otherwise the early-exit value, equal to Lower_Bound.

Lower_Bound

OpenCV's weighted-center bound when Lower_Bound_Available is True: the Metric distance between the weight-scaled coordinate sums of the two signatures divided by the larger native mass. It is 0.0 when no bound is available.

Lower_Bound_Available

True when the native Float32 masses satisfy abs (Sum_1 - Sum_2) < 1.0e-5 * Sum_1, so OpenCV computed the bound.

Exact_Distance_Computed

True when the native transport solve ran; False only when an available bound reached the threshold.

Earth_Mover_Distance

function Earth_Mover_Distance
  (Signature_1, Signature_2 : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Metric                   : Earth_Mover_Metric := Euclidean_EMD)
   return OpenCV.Float32_Value

Exact Earth Mover's Distance between two weighted signatures under a built-in Metric, computed by native cv::EMD with a full transport solve.

Nonempty 2-D Float32 C1 signatures: each row is (weight, coordinates). Built-in metrics require at least one coordinate; both signatures always have equal column counts. All weights are finite and nonnegative, each signature has positive total mass, and its sum must remain finite in Float32. Built-in coordinates are finite; relevant transport costs (between rows of positive weight, including every Float32 coordinate difference and the Float32 L2 squared sum) are finite, nonnegative and strictly less than the native 1.0e20 Float32 sentinel. Unequal total masses are balanced by an internal zero-cost dummy cluster; distance is normalized by the larger mass. Inputs, including Regions, are deep-snapshotted into packed storage before the native solve and are never modified. No lower-bound shortcut is used. Signature sizes whose OpenCV 4.1 work buffer or signed row indexing would overflow int (for example two 16_000-row signatures) are rejected. A nonfinite native distance is not returned.

Parameters
Signature_1

First signature; its rows index the rows of a transport flow.

Signature_2

Second signature, with Signature_1's column count.

Metric

Ground distance between coordinate vectors; defaults to Euclidean_EMD.

Return Value

The native Float32 distance.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid signature shape or type, unequal column counts, an invalid weight, mass, coordinate or cost, a native size limit, a nonfinite result, or a native failure.

Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Cost

function Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Cost
  (Signature_1, Signature_2, Cost : OpenCV.Core.Mat)
   return OpenCV.Float32_Value

Exact Earth Mover's Distance with an explicit ground-cost matrix instead of a built-in metric (native DIST_USER), always solved in full. Explicit Cost permits weights-only signatures (one column); Cost is Float32 C1, with Rows = Signature_1.Rows and Columns = Signature_2.Rows. The element at row I, column J is the cost of moving mass from row I of Signature_1 to row J of Signature_2. All supplied cost entries are checked, including entries for zero-weight rows: each must be finite, nonnegative and strictly less than the native 1.0e20 Float32 sentinel. Coordinate columns, when present, are ignored, but both signatures still need equal column counts. Weight, mass, normalization, native size and snapshot rules (Cost included) are those of Earth_Mover_Distance.

Parameters
Signature_1

First signature; its rows index the Cost rows.

Signature_2

Second signature, with Signature_1's column count; its rows index the Cost columns.

Cost

Nonempty 2-D Float32 C1 transport-cost matrix of Signature_1.Rows x Signature_2.Rows; it is not modified.

Return Value

The native Float32 distance.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid signature or Cost shape or type, unequal column counts, an invalid weight, mass or cost entry, a native size limit, a nonfinite result, or a native failure.

Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Cost_And_Flow

function Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Cost_And_Flow
  (Signature_1, Signature_2, Cost : OpenCV.Core.Mat)
   return Earth_Mover_Result

As Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Cost (same signature, Cost, safety and snapshot contract, always an exact native solve), also returning the optimal flow. Flow is a fresh Float32 C1 Rows_1 x Rows_2 Mat of real transported mass; dummy mass is not represented. Distance and Flow are published only after success.

Parameters
Signature_1

First signature; its rows index the Cost and Flow rows.

Signature_2

Second signature, with Signature_1's column count; its rows index the Cost and Flow columns.

Cost

Nonempty 2-D Float32 C1 transport-cost matrix of Signature_1.Rows x Signature_2.Rows; it is not modified.

Return Value

Distance and fresh owning Flow (see Earth_Mover_Result).

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for the conditions listed for Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Cost.

Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Flow

function Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Flow
  (Signature_1, Signature_2 : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Metric                   : Earth_Mover_Metric := Euclidean_EMD)
   return Earth_Mover_Result

As Earth_Mover_Distance (same signature, metric, safety and snapshot contract, always an exact native solve), also returning the optimal flow. Flow is a fresh Float32 C1 Rows_1 x Rows_2 Mat of real transported mass; dummy mass is not represented. Distance and Flow are published only after success.

Parameters
Signature_1

First signature; its rows index the Flow rows.

Signature_2

Second signature, with Signature_1's column count; its rows index the Flow columns.

Metric

Ground distance between coordinate vectors; defaults to Euclidean_EMD.

Return Value

Distance and fresh owning Flow (see Earth_Mover_Result).

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for the conditions listed for Earth_Mover_Distance.

Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Lower_Bound

function Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Lower_Bound
  (Signature_1, Signature_2 : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Metric                   : Earth_Mover_Metric := Euclidean_EMD;
   Early_Exit_Threshold     : OpenCV.Float32_Value :=
     OpenCV.Float32_Value'Last) return Earth_Mover_Bounded_Result

Same signature and native-safety contract as exact built-in EMD above. Threshold must be finite and nonnegative. When native Float32 masses satisfy abs (Sum_1 - Sum_2) < 1.0e-5 * Sum_1, OpenCV calculates its weighted-center bound. If Bound >= Threshold, it skips transport: Distance = Lower_Bound and Exact_Distance_Computed = False. Equality exits too; zero requests bound-only whenever available. Default largest finite Float32 requests exact distance plus the bound. Unequal masses: Available = False, Bound = 0, exact solve still runs. Full safety preflight and packed Region snapshots precede the shortcut. Nonfinite native results raise OpenCV_Error. No Cost overload (native bound requires a metric) or Flow (early-exit zeros are not transport). When the bound is available, checks guarding OpenCV 4.1's bound arithmetic also reject a signature whose Rows * Columns exceeds 2_147_483_647, or signatures whose bound scratch would not fit the native work buffer (for example very wide one-row signatures). A negative or nonfinite available bound is an error. Inputs are never modified.

Parameters
Signature_1

First signature, as for Earth_Mover_Distance; its mass is the reference of the availability tolerance.

Signature_2

Second signature, with Signature_1's column count.

Metric

Ground distance used for transport and for the bound; defaults to Euclidean_EMD.

Early_Exit_Threshold

Finite, nonnegative threshold compared with an available bound; the default OpenCV.Float32_Value'Last computes the exact distance and still reports the bound.

Return Value

Distance, bound and the two availability and exactness flags.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for a negative or nonfinite Early_Exit_Threshold, any condition listed for Earth_Mover_Distance, a bound-path native layout limit, or a negative or nonfinite native bound.

Earth_Mover_Metric

type Earth_Mover_Metric is (Manhattan_EMD, Euclidean_EMD, Chessboard_EMD);

Built-in ground distance between the coordinate vectors of two signature rows, used by the Earth Mover's Distance functions. OpenCV subtracts coordinates in Float32, accumulates in Float64 and returns a Float32 cost.

Enumeration Literal
Manhattan_EMD

L1 distance (OpenCV DIST_L1): the sum of absolute coordinate differences.

Euclidean_EMD

L2 distance (OpenCV DIST_L2): the square root of the sum of squared differences, narrowed to Float32 before the root. This is the default metric.

Chessboard_EMD

Chebyshev distance (OpenCV DIST_C): the largest absolute coordinate difference.

Earth_Mover_Result

type Earth_Mover_Result is record
   Distance : OpenCV.Float32_Value;
   Flow     : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
end record;

Exact transport result of Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Flow and Earth_Mover_Distance_With_Cost_And_Flow.

Record fields
Distance

Native Float32 Earth Mover's Distance, normalized by the larger total signature mass.

Flow

Fresh owning Float32 C1 Mat with Signature_1.Rows rows and Signature_2.Rows columns. The element at row I, column J is the real mass moved from row I of Signature_1 to row J of Signature_2; mass exchanged with the internal dummy cluster is not represented, and zero-weight rows carry no flow.

Encode_YUV420_Planar

function Encode_YUV420_Planar
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Layout : YUV420_Planar_Layout;
   Order  : YUV_Color_Order := BGR_Order) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Encodes a color image as a packed planar YUV 4:2:0 frame with native cv::cvtColor. Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C3/C4, even W/H >=2. BGR_Order means BGR/BGRA; RGB_Order means RGB/RGBA. Alpha is ignored, not premultiplied. Native encoding computes Y per pixel and samples U/V at the top-left pixel of each 2x2 block, without averaging. Subsampling is lossy. The native BT.601 limited-range encoder maps black to Y,U,V 16,128,128 and white to 235,128,128.

Source is copied into an independent packed snapshot first, so a Region is encoded as its own frame and Source is unchanged. NV12/NV21 encoding is not provided.

Parameters
Source

Color image: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C3 or C4 of H rows and W columns, with W and H even and at least 2.

Layout

Plane order of the result: I420 or YV12.

Order

Channel order of Source; BGR_Order by default.

Return Value

Fresh owning packed frame: UInt8 C1 of W columns and H + H/2 rows.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D, not UInt8, or not C3 or C4; when its row or column count is odd or below 2; when a native signed-arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded (for example W * H above 2_147_483_647); or when OpenCV fails.

Equalize_Histogram

procedure Equalize_Histogram
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat; Destination : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat)

Equalize_Histogram performs global histogram equalization of a grayscale UInt8 image. Source must be a non-empty two-dimensional single-channel UInt8 Mat. There is no implicit conversion from color or from another depth. The histogram is computed over the supplied Mat view, not over an enclosing parent image outside that view. Constant images retain their intensity. Destination may initially be empty or have a different size, depth, or channel count; on success it receives Source's rows and columns with UInt8 depth and one channel. OpenCV writes Destination directly: it reuses Destination's storage when Destination already is a UInt8 C1 Mat of Source's size (shallow copies of Destination and a Region's parent then see the result) and otherwise rebinds Destination to new storage. Source remains unchanged when Source and Destination do not share storage. Direct same-object in-place use is supported:

Equalize_Histogram (Image, Image);

Other Source/Destination storage-sharing combinations are an Ada binding restriction for this slice and are rejected before native writes. That includes distinct shallow aliases and partially overlapping source and destination ROIs; it is not a claim that OpenCV forbids every alias. Source.Rows times Source.Columns must not exceed 2_147_483_647 because OpenCV counts pixels in 32-bit histogram bins. Public contract violations raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error before Destination is modified. Failures reported by OpenCV after Destination allocation or native execution raise OpenCV.OpenCV_Error and do not roll back Destination.

Parameters
Source

Nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 image whose own pixels define the histogram; it is not modified unless it is Destination itself.

Destination

Receives the equalized UInt8 C1 image of Source's size; may be Source itself but must not otherwise share storage with Source.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an empty, non-2-D, non-UInt8 or multi-channel Source, a pixel count above 2_147_483_647, a Destination that shares storage with a distinct Source, or a native failure.

Erode

procedure Erode
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the centered Kernel_Size Erode, but with a caller-supplied structuring element anchored at (Kernel.Columns / 2, Kernel.Rows / 2). Custom kernels are nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 binary masks: zero excludes, every nonzero value includes, without weighting. All-zero masks are rejected. Even-sized, non-square and non-contiguous Region kernels are accepted; only the kernel's logical view participates and Kernel is not modified. Kernel and Destination must not overlap. Source/Destination in-place is supported. Regions use only their logical views, including parent-backed in-place. Border, Destination and native-limit behavior are those of the Kernel_Size overload; the expanded-rectangle limit applies when every Kernel element is nonzero.

Parameters
Source

Image to erode.

Destination

Receives the eroded image (direct native output with Source's geometry and element type).

Kernel

Structuring element: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask whose nonzero elements are included.

Iterations

Number of successive erosions; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source or Kernel, an all-zero Kernel, Kernel storage overlapping Destination, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Erode

procedure Erode
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Anchor       : OpenCV.Point;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the custom-Kernel Erode, but with an explicit Anchor inside Kernel, where (0, 0) is the top-left kernel element; no negative center sentinel exists. The kernel's footprint shifts with Anchor. Kernel requirements, overlap rejection, Border, Destination, in-place, Region and native-limit behavior are those of the centered overload.

Parameters
Source

Image to erode.

Destination

Receives the eroded image (direct native output with Source's geometry and element type).

Kernel

Structuring element: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 mask whose nonzero elements are included.

Anchor

Kernel position aligned with each output pixel; it must lie inside Kernel.

Iterations

Number of successive erosions; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source or Kernel, an all-zero Kernel, an Anchor outside Kernel, Kernel storage overlapping Destination, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Erode

procedure Erode
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel_Size  : OpenCV.Size;
   Shape        : Morphology_Shape := Rectangle;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

Erode replaces Destination with the neighborhood minimum selected by a temporary structuring element of Kernel_Size and Shape, generated as by Get_Structuring_Element and anchored at (Width / 2, Height / 2). Source must be a non-empty two-dimensional Mat of depth UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32, or Float64; channels are processed independently. Kernel dimensions must be positive, but need not be odd, and Iterations is at least one. Constant_Border, Replicate, Reflect, and Reflect_101 are supported; Wrap is rejected. Constant_Border uses Border_Value, by default OpenCV's neutral morphology border value.

Destination receives Source's geometry and element type as a direct native output: OpenCV writes into its storage when it already has that size and type, so shallow copies and a Region's parent see the result, and otherwise rebinds it to new storage. Source remains unchanged unless it is also Destination, which is supported for in-place erosion; for a parent-backed Region only the view's pixels change. A Region is processed as its own logical image; pixels in its parent outside the view do not participate.

Native signed-int limits apply: the kernel area, Source columns times channels and kernel width times channels must fit a 32-bit int, and a fully nonzero kernel with Iterations above one must also fit OpenCV's expanded-rectangle and anchor arithmetic. Ellipse height is limited to 92681. A 1 x 1 kernel copies Source for any Iterations.

Parameters
Source

Image to erode.

Destination

Receives the eroded image.

Kernel_Size

Width (columns) and height (rows) of the generated structuring element; both must be positive.

Shape

Generated element shape; defaults to Rectangle.

Iterations

Number of successive erosions; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, a zero kernel dimension, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Erode

procedure Erode
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Destination  : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Kernel_Size  : OpenCV.Size;
   Shape        : Morphology_Shape;
   Anchor       : OpenCV.Point;
   Iterations   : Morphology_Iterations := 1;
   Border       : OpenCV.Border_Kind := OpenCV.Constant_Border;
   Border_Value : Morphology_Border_Value := Default_Morphology_Border)

As the centered Kernel_Size Erode, but with an explicit Anchor inside the Kernel_Size box, where (0, 0) is the top-left element pixel; no negative center sentinel exists. An explicit Cross anchor also changes the generated mask geometry: its row and column pass through Anchor. Rectangle and Ellipse masks do not depend on Anchor, but their footprint shifts with it. Source, Destination, border, in-place, Region and native-limit behavior are those of the centered overload.

Parameters
Source

Image to erode.

Destination

Receives the eroded image (direct native output with Source's geometry and element type).

Kernel_Size

Width (columns) and height (rows) of the generated structuring element; both must be positive.

Shape

Generated element shape; no default in this overload.

Anchor

Element position aligned with each output pixel; it must lie inside the element.

Iterations

Number of successive erosions; defaults to 1.

Border

Extrapolation of pixels outside Source; defaults to Constant_Border.

Border_Value

Constant border value, used only with Constant_Border; defaults to Default_Morphology_Border.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised for an invalid Source, a zero kernel dimension, an Anchor outside the element, Wrap, an invalid explicit Border_Value, a native arithmetic limit, or an OpenCV failure.

Explicit_Morphology_Border

function Explicit_Morphology_Border
  (Value : OpenCV.Scalar) return Morphology_Border_Value

Selects an ordinary constant border value for Constant_Border morphology; it is ignored for other borders. For C1 .. C4 Sources, components 0 .. Channels - 1 map to the channels; Sources with more than four channels require all four components equal. The value is checked when an operation runs: the components used must be finite, Float32 Sources need them within the finite Float32 range, and integer Sources need them within the signed-int range -2_147_483_648 .. 2_147_483_647 before OpenCV rounds and saturates them to the pixel range (300 becomes 255 for UInt8, not a wrapped value). The all-DBL_MAX scalar is reserved by OpenCV for its neutral default and is rejected here.

Parameters
Value

Border value in channel order; not every component equal to Float64_Value'Last (native DBL_MAX).

Return Value

Explicit border value wrapping Value.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when every component of Value equals Float64_Value'Last.

Extract_Subpixel_Patch

function Extract_Subpixel_Patch
  (Source       : OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Patch_Size   : OpenCV.Size;
   Center       : OpenCV.Float32_Point;
   Output_Depth : Subpixel_Patch_Output_Depth := Preserve_Source_Depth)
   return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Native bilinear extraction (cv::getRectSubPix) of a patch centered at a subpixel Center, with nearest-edge extension. Channels are interpolated independently; patch pixel (R, C) samples Source at

X = Center.X - (Patch_Size.Width - 1) / 2 + C
Y = Center.Y - (Patch_Size.Height - 1) / 2 + R

Source must be nonempty 2-D UInt8/Float32 C1/C3. Patch dimensions must be positive; they may exceed Source. Center must be finite and inside Source (0 <= X < Columns, 0 <= Y < Rows), and is Region-local. Source values may be nonfinite and propagate natively into the samples that use them. UInt8 results use native fixed-point rounding. Source is unchanged and a Region's parent pixels never participate: requests touching the border sample a private replicated guard copy with the same logical geometry. A patch crossing both the top and the right border is sampled by OpenCV's portable implementation even where Intel IPP is enabled, whose corner replication there is wrong. Arithmetic and backend representability limits can reject extreme dimensions or strides.

Parameters
Source

Image to sample.

Patch_Size

Patch columns (Width) and rows (Height); both nonzero.

Center

Patch center in Source pixel coordinates (X = column, Y = row).

Output_Depth

Result depth; default Preserve_Source_Depth.

Return Value

Fresh independent owning Mat with Patch_Size.Height rows, Patch_Size.Width columns, Source's channel count and the depth selected by Output_Depth.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Unsupported Source, zero Patch_Size dimension, nonfinite or outside Center, native arithmetic or stride limits, or an OpenCV failure.

Extract_YUV420_Luma

function Extract_YUV420_Luma
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Copies the luma plane of a packed YUV 4:2:0 frame of any layout. Raw Y bytes, UInt8 C1 H x W, with no subtract-16 or rescaling. Chroma does not participate. Source is validated as for Decode_YUV420 and copied into an independent packed snapshot first; a packed Region must itself contain a complete standalone frame.

Parameters
Source

Packed frame: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C1 with an even column count of at least 2 and a row count that is a positive multiple of 3.

Return Value

Fresh owning UInt8 C1 Mat of H rows (two thirds of Source's rows) and W columns: the first H rows of Source, byte for byte.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D or not UInt8 C1; when its column count is odd or below 2 or its row count is not a positive multiple of 3; when a native arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded; or when OpenCV fails.

Extract_YUV422_Luma

function Extract_YUV422_Luma
  (Source : OpenCV.Core.Mat; Layout : YUV422_Layout) return OpenCV.Core.Mat

Exact raw Y bytes, C1, no subtract-16, scaling or chroma participation. UYVY uses source channel 1; YUY2/YVYU use channel 0. The frame contract is the same as for Decode_YUV422: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C2 with an even width, copied by logical rows into an independent snapshot, with a Region treated as a standalone frame in the caller's byte phase. YUV422 encoding is intentionally deferred: OpenCV 4.1 lacks it.

Parameters
Source

Packed frame: nonempty 2-D UInt8 C2 with at least one row and an even column count of at least 2.

Layout

Byte order of Source, which selects the Y channel.

Return Value

Fresh owning UInt8 C1 Mat with Source's rows and columns holding each pixel's Y byte unchanged.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Source is empty, not 2-D or not UInt8 C2; when its column count is odd or below 2; when a native arithmetic or address-span limit is exceeded (for example 2 * W above 2_147_483_647); or when OpenCV fails.

Fill_Circle

procedure Fill_Circle
  (Image      : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Center     : OpenCV.Point;
   Radius     : Drawing_Radius;
   Color      : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Line_Style : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line)

Fills the disc of Radius around Center, including Center, in Image in place with cv::circle in OpenCV's filled mode, under the package drawing rules.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Center

Disc center; may lie outside Image.

Radius

Disc radius in pixels.

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Line_Style

Edge rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, and when OpenCV fails.

Fill_Contours

procedure Fill_Contours
  (Image             : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Contours          : Contour_Set;
   Root              : Contour_Index;
   Color             : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Descendant_Levels : Natural := 0;
   Line_Style        : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line;
   Offset            : OpenCV.Point := (X => 0, Y => 0))

Fills the contour Root and up to Descendant_Levels generations of its descendants into Image in place, with even-odd parity over that selection; otherwise as the all-contours Fill_Contours. With zero levels Root is filled including the area of its holes, one level empties its child holes, and two refills islands inside them. Contours are selected in Ada, independently of the linked OpenCV version, by following First_Child and Next links rather than storage order; a depth beyond the tree includes every reachable descendant. Contours without points are skipped. Offset shifts points in image/Region-local coordinates. Image and Color obey the ordinary drawing contract. Structural errors, including an invalid Root, leave Image unchanged; native drawing errors need not roll back.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Contours

Contour set containing Root, normally from Find_Contours.

Root

Index of the selected subtree's root; must be less than Contour_Count (Contours).

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Descendant_Levels

Generations below Root to include: zero selects Root only, one adds its children, two also adds grandchildren. Defaults to 0.

Line_Style

Edge rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Offset

Added to every point before rasterization, in Image (Region-local) coordinates; defaults to (0, 0).

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Root is not less than Contour_Count (Contours), when the hierarchy reached from Root is inconsistent, when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, when the selected point count or a point plus Offset exceeds the signed 32-bit range, and when OpenCV fails.

Fill_Contours

procedure Fill_Contours
  (Image      : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Contours   : Contour_Set;
   Color      : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Line_Style : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line;
   Offset     : OpenCV.Point := (X => 0, Y => 0))

Fills every contour stored in Contours into Image in place with cv::drawContours in OpenCV's filled mode, under the package drawing rules. Filled contours use even-odd parity over the whole selection: an outer contour alone fills its interior, an added hole remains empty, and an island inside the hole is filled again, so holes and islands of one Find_Contours result are preserved. Select contours in Ada, independently of the linked OpenCV version: all-contours overloads use every stored contour (the Root overloads select a subtree), and contours without points are skipped. Offset shifts points in image/Region-local coordinates. Image and Color obey the ordinary drawing contract, including UInt8-only Anti_Aliased_Line. When no selected contour has points the call is a no-op after Image, Color and Line_Style validation. Structural errors leave Image unchanged; native drawing errors need not roll back.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Contours

Contour set to fill, normally from Find_Contours.

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Line_Style

Edge rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Offset

Added to every point before rasterization, in Image (Region-local) coordinates; defaults to (0, 0).

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, when the selected point or contour count exceeds the signed 32-bit range, when a point plus Offset leaves that range, and when OpenCV fails.

Fill_Ellipse

procedure Fill_Ellipse
  (Image       : in out OpenCV.Core.Mat;
   Center      : OpenCV.Point;
   Axes        : OpenCV.Size;
   Angle       : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Start_Angle : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   End_Angle   : OpenCV.Float64_Value;
   Color       : OpenCV.Scalar;
   Line_Style  : Drawing_Line_Style := Eight_Connected_Line;
   Units       : OpenCV.Angle_Unit := OpenCV.Degrees)

Fills an ellipse or elliptic sector in Image in place with cv::ellipse in OpenCV's filled mode, under the package drawing rules. The geometry is that of Draw_Ellipse. Fill_Ellipse fills the elliptic sector for a partial interval and the whole ellipse, including Center, for a full turn. Axes are the ellipse semi-axis lengths and must both be positive. Angle, Start_Angle, and End_Angle must be finite. Radians are converted to degrees in Ada; values are not otherwise normalized.

Parameters
Image

Target modified in place: a nonempty 2-D UInt8, UInt16, Int16, Float32 or Float64 Mat or Region with 1 to 4 channels.

Center

Ellipse center; may lie outside Image.

Axes

Semi-axis lengths in pixels, Width for the first axis and Height for the second; both must be positive.

Angle

Rotation of the ellipse, in Units; must be finite.

Start_Angle

Start of the sector, in Units; must be finite.

End_Angle

End of the sector, in Units; must be finite.

Color

Value written in channel order; the components used by Image's channels must be finite.

Line_Style

Edge rasterization; defaults to Eight_Connected_Line. Anti_Aliased_Line requires a UInt8 Image.

Units

Unit of Angle, Start_Angle and End_Angle; defaults to Degrees.

Raised exceptions
OpenCV_Error

Raised when an axis is zero, when an angle is not finite, when Image or a used Color component violates the drawing rules, when Anti_Aliased_Line is used with a non-UInt8 Image, and when OpenCV fails.

Fill_Rectangle

procedure Fill_Rectangle

   Color      : OpenCV.Scalar;

Fills the rectangle Bounds in Image in place with cv::rectangle in OpenCV's filled mode, under the package drawing rules. Every pixel of columns Bounds.X .. Bounds.X + Bounds.Width - 1 and rows Bounds.Y .. Bounds.Y + Bounds.Height - 1 inside Image is painted. Width and Height must be positive. As for Draw_Rectangle, the far corner is saturated, so huge extents are clipped.
